Conclusion: PCH通过模拟人类视觉系统的感知机制，在立体全景图像质量评估中实现了高效且准确的盲/无参考评估，具备良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Stereoscopic omnidirectional images (SOIs) have provided users with newly immersive quality of experience in virtual reality environments. However, developing efficient and accurate perceptual quality assessment metrics for SOIs remains challenging due to many factors such as freely changeable field of views and binocular vision. In this paper, based on the characteristics of the human visual system (HVS), we propose a Predictive Coding Hierarchy-inspired metric (PCH) for blind/no-reference stereoscopic omnidirectional image quality assessment. Motivated by the viewing process of SOIs, the proposed PCH includes a local cyclopean perception module, a global predictive perception module, and a visual quality regressor. First, observers browse different spherical sceneries from viewports, and aggregate the local visual information to infer the perceptual quality of SOIs. Therefore, we extract various viewports, followed by cyclopean conversion and saliency detection to approach the perception and attention of the human brain. After the local aggregation, viewers then infer the global scene in their minds. Based on the binocular mechanism, we fuse left and right views to perform predictive coding hierarchy modelling. Finally, the visual quality regressor is exploited to obtain the ultimate quality score related to both local and global perceptual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PCH achieves competitive and consistently improved performance compared with state-of-the-art quality assessment methods.

</details>


### [35] [A Large-scale Evaluation of Text-guided Models for Facial Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28802)
*Rahul Nair,Saurav Pandit,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对六种主流文本引导模型在面部编辑任务中进行了大规模评估（约100万张图像），构建了包含169个面部属性的Face-Edit-Attributes数据集，专注于发型、配饰和姿态编辑。结果显示，大多数模型在发型和配饰编辑上表现良好，但在姿态编辑上表现不佳，且普遍存在过度编辑问题（如仅要求改变发型却同时改变了发色）。此外，模型在深肤色男性及老年人群中表现出显著的过度编辑偏差。代码与数据已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在面部编辑中存在局限：GANs易产生不稳定的编辑结果，3DMMs只能调整姿态和表情，而文本引导扩散模型虽具潜力，但尚未系统评估其在面部编辑中的性能与公平性。

Method: 通过构建大规模面部编辑数据集Face-Edit-Attributes，使用CelebA和CelebSET两个知名人脸数据集，对六种主流文本引导模型进行序列化面部编辑任务评估，量化其在发型、配饰、姿态等属性上的表现，并分析模型在不同人群中的偏见。

Result: 多数模型在发型和配饰编辑上表现良好，但在姿态编辑上表现差；所有模型均存在过度编辑现象，尤其在深肤色男性和老年人群中更为严重。

Conclusion: 文本引导模型虽在面部编辑中展现出潜力，但存在显著的性能瓶颈与伦理风险，需在设计与应用中关注公平性与可控性。

Abstract: Facial appearance editing powers popular applications like FaceApp and Photoshop. Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) have been widely used for facial editing. GANs can perform varied facial edits (e.g., changing hair color, hairstyle), but often produce unstable edits. 3DMMs produce stable edits, but can only alter pose and facial expression. Recently, text-guided diffusion models like Nano Banana have become popular for image editing. Text-guided models are a compelling alternative to GANs and 3DMMs since they can produce both stable and varied image edits. While text-guided models have been widely tested for whole-scene edits (e.g., ``make the woman play a guitar''), they have not been comprehensively tested for facial editing. We conducted the first large-scale evaluation ($\sim1$M images evaluated) of six popular text-guided models on a sequential facial editing task. We present Face-Edit-Attributes, the largest collection of $169$ facial editing attributes focused on hair, accessories, and pose edits. We compared model performance using two popular celebrity face datasets: CelebA and CelebSET. Our results show that most models performed hair and accessory edits well, but struggled with editing pose. All models over-edit (e.g., changing hair color when asked only to change the hairstyle). We also evaluated demographic biases in each model. Our results show surprising biases in overediting: almost all models created more overedits for dark-skinned male faces and old faces. The code and data for our results (including our repository of $\sim 1$M images) can be accessed \href{https://github.com/rahul1801/Face-Edit-Bench}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [36] [FigMirror: Ground It, Code It, Plot It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28814)
*Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FigMirror是一种基于坐标定位与代码执行能力的智能框架，用于将科学图表的视觉风格迁移到新数据上。它通过Grounded Measurement技术精准识别图表元素并测量其属性，克服了传统方法依赖像素级优化的局限性。研究还构建了PlotTwin-Bench基准测试集，包含细粒度代码与图像风格指标，实验表明该方法在参考条件下的风格迁移任务中显著优于现有方法。所有图表均由FigMirror生成，代码与数据已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注复现参考图本身，难以将图表的视觉风格（如配色、字体）有效迁移到新数据上，且依赖像素级优化导致泛化能力差。因此需要一种能够理解并迁移图表风格的新方法。

Method: 提出FigMirror框架，利用现代计算机使用模型的坐标定位与代码生成能力，通过Grounded Measurement技术以坐标为基础定位视觉元素并测量其属性，实现对图表风格的精确捕捉与迁移。同时构建PlotTwin-Bench基准，支持细粒度评估。

Result: FigMirror在参考条件下的风格迁移任务中表现优异，能有效将参考图的视觉风格应用于新数据，并在多个指标上超越现有方法；所有实验图表均由该框架生成。

Conclusion: FigMirror通过坐标引导的代码化分析实现了科学图表风格的有效迁移，展示了大型模型在图形理解与生成中的潜力，为自动化科研可视化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Converting scientific figures into executable code has gained increasing attention, yet existing methods primarily focus on reproducing the reference figure itself. A more practical setting is to plot new data while preserving the visual style of a reference figure (e.g., color scheme and typography). Prior approaches mimic the reference through pixel-level optimization and struggle to carry its style to new data. We show that the key to this task lies in the coordinate grounding and coding capabilities present in modern computer-use models. We propose FigMirror, an agentic framework that unlocks these capabilities through Grounded Measurement, which locates visual elements by coordinates and measures their properties through executable code. We further introduce PlotTwin-Bench, an expert-curated benchmark with fine-grained code and image-level style metrics. Experiments show that FigMirror consistently outperforms existing methods on reference-conditioned style transfer. All plots in this paper are generated by FigMirror, except those produced by other methods for comparison. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror.

</details>


### [37] [Text-Driven Artistic Staging: Pose, Lighting, and Camera References from Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28823)
*Yunge Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种文本到可编辑3D布景的新任务，联合生成人体姿态、主光源和相机配置，以响应情感化描述。通过分析2,328幅具象绘画，构建了11,911个文本-布景对，并使用流匹配变换器模型支持多角色及多种布景方案生成。在保留语料库多样性的同时，模型在测试集上达到32.2%的R@1检索准确率，显著优于基于CLIP的最近邻方法（16.6%），验证了从文本生成可编辑、情绪化3D布景的可行性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法通常独立建模人体姿态、光照和相机设置，无法有效表达艺术中的叙事与情感；本文旨在通过联合建模这些元素，实现从情感化文本生成具有艺术表现力的3D布景。

Method: 从具象绘画中重建SMPL人体模型，估计低频光照，恢复相机参数，并结合ArtEmis描述构建文本-布景配对数据集；采用流匹配变换器模型，支持可变数量的人物并生成多种布景选项。

Result: 模型在未见描述上的检索R@1达到32.2%，优于基于CLIP的最近邻方法（16.6%），同时保持了语料库级别的多样性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了从文本生成可编辑、情绪驱动的3D布景是可行的，为艺术创作与视觉生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Artists coordinate human pose, illumination, and camera placement to convey narrative and emotion, but existing generative methods typically model these elements independently. We introduce text-to-editable 3D staging, a task that jointly generates human poses, a dominant light, and a camera configuration from an affective description. We construct 11,911 text--staging pairs from 2,328 figurative paintings by reconstructing SMPL bodies, estimating low-frequency illumination, recovering camera parameters, and pairing each scene with ArtEmis descriptions. We train a flow-matching transformer that supports variable numbers of figures and produces multiple staging alternatives for each prompt. On held-out descriptions, the model achieves 32.2\% retrieval R@1, compared with 16.6\% for CLIP-based nearest-neighbor retrieval, while approximately preserving corpus-level diversity. These results demonstrate the feasibility of generating editable, emotionally conditioned 3D staging references from text.

</details>


### [38] [BlobBoards: Robust Markers for Accurate Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28830)
*James Pritts,Till Sittart,Hendrik Sauer,Silja Janßen,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlobBoards是一种基于高斯斑点的密集多尺度标记系统，结合基于特征的检测、识别和姿态估计流程。通过数百个斑点特征的密集空间覆盖来约束姿态，并利用多尺度设计确保在焦距、距离和视角大幅变化下仍可检测。采用学习的局部描述符匹配参考模式并进行空间验证，从而确定姿态并确认身份。实验表明，与运动捕捉真值相比，其平均平移误差为3.6-5.0毫米，较AprilTag在小板上减少89%，在大板上减少70%；且大幅减少大角度旋转失败。在检测率方面达到80%，优于AprilTag的74%和ArUco的58%，尤其在最小标记上优势显著。在50%遮挡下仍能保持69%的检测率，且平均平移误差几乎不变，而AprilTag和ArUco则完全无法检测。总体表现优于现有最先进标记系统，在检测率、姿态精度和遮挡鲁棒性方面均达领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标记系统在小尺寸、大视角变化、遮挡等复杂条件下存在检测率低、姿态估计误差大、旋转失败频发等问题，亟需一种兼具高精度、高鲁棒性和强适应性的新型标记系统。

Method: 提出一种由密集多尺度高斯斑点构成的标记板（BlobBoards），通过特征提取与匹配、空间一致性验证实现联合检测、识别与姿态估计。利用多尺度斑点设计增强跨条件可检测性，采用学习的局部描述符提高匹配准确性，并通过几何约束提升姿态估计稳定性。

Result: 在多种挑战性条件下，BlobBoards表现出卓越性能：平均平移误差降低89%（小板）至70%（大板），检测率达80%（高于AprilTag的74%和ArUco的58%），在50%遮挡下仍保持69%检测率且误差稳定，大幅减少大角度旋转失败。

Conclusion: BlobBoards在检测率、姿态精度和遮挡鲁棒性方面均达到当前最优水平，是适用于复杂视觉场景下的高性能标记系统。

Abstract: We propose BlobBoards, a fiducial marker system comprising a dense, multi-scale field of Gaussian blobs and a feature-based pipeline for joint detection, identification, and pose estimation. Each board is registered from hundreds of blob features whose dense spatial coverage constrains pose, while multiple scales preserve detectability across large changes in focal length, distance, and obliquity. Learned local descriptors are matched to the reference pattern and spatially verified, so the correspondences determine pose and certify identity. Against motion-capture ground truth, BlobBoards achieve median translation errors of 3.6-5.0 mm, reducing AprilTag's median translation error by 89% on small boards and 70% on large ones. They also produce far fewer large-rotation failures than state-of-the-art tag systems. BlobBoards achieve the highest detection rate, 80% versus 74% for AprilTag and 58% for ArUco, with the largest margin on the smallest markers. Under 50% occlusion, they still detect 69% of boards with essentially unchanged median translation error, while AprilTag and ArUco detect none. In experiments BlobBoards give state-of-the-art detection rate, pose accuracy and occlusion robustness.

</details>


### [39] [MWIR-4-Plastic: The Identification of Complex End-of-Life Industrial Plastic using Mid-wave Infrared Hyperspectral Imaging and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28874)
*Elias Arbash,Andréa de Lima Ribeiro,Filipa Simões,Ahmed Jamal Afifi,Aldino Rizaldy,Yuleika Madriz,Samuel Thiele,Sandra Lorenz,Margret Fuchs,Pedram Ghamisi,Paul Scheunders,Richard Gloaguen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个公开的废旧工业车辆中碎裂黑色塑料的高光谱成像（HSI）数据集，涵盖四种工业聚合物，包含RGB、VNIR、SWIR和MWIR多模态场景。研究构建了融合前景分离、像素级分类与对象级多数投票的多模态光谱-空间框架，结合先进的高光谱变换器与化学计量波段选择，在复杂黑色塑料分类中实现高精度。通过九种方法对比，验证了该框架在工业检测中的有效性，并公开全部数据与方法以促进可复现性，建立首个针对工业废料中高光谱物体分析的基准体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回收设施难以自动化分拣碎裂黑色塑料，因当前传感技术多依赖单点中红外光谱或实验室型高光谱成像，缺乏空间分辨能力，且数据集多基于完整塑料，不反映实际破碎状态；同时，现有分类方法依赖人工区域选取和规则匹配，忽视空间信息，未采用现代深度学习技术，限制了回收效率与精度提升。

Method: 提出多模态光谱-空间分析框架，包括：1）前景分离以提取待检对象；2）基于高光谱变换器的像素级分类；3）对象级多数投票进行最终判定；结合化学计量学波段选择优化输入特征，实现高效准确分类。

Result: 所提框架在复杂碎裂黑色塑料分类任务中表现优异，优于传统化学计量、机器学习及多种深度学习模型；建立了首个全面基准，验证了多模态融合与深度学习在工业高光谱分析中的潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了碎裂黑色塑料高光谱数据与分析方法的空白，提出的公开数据集与可复现框架为工业废弃物智能分拣提供了重要基础，推动了高光谱技术在循环经济中的应用发展。

Abstract: The automated sorting of shredded black plastics from end-of-life (EOF) industrial waste presents a significant challenge in recycling facilities, primarily due to the limitations of current sensing and analytical approaches. Existing studies predominantly rely on single-point contact-based mid-infrared spectroscopy or laboratory hyperspectral imaging (HSI) setups, which fail to provide the spatially resolved analysis necessary for fast, bulk processing. Moreover, available datasets are laboratory-controlled and focus on intact rather than shredded plastics, hindering further recycling refinement. Black industrial plastics, in particular, are underrepresented, while most classification pipelines depend on manual region selection and rule-based spectral matching, neglecting spatial information and modern deep learning (DL) methods. To address these gaps, we introduce the first publicly available HSI dataset of shredded black plastics from EOF vehicle, comprising four industrial polymers across 13 co-registered RGB, VNIR, SWIR, and MWIR scenes and their segmentation pipeline. We developed a multi-modal spectral-spatial framework that integrates foreground isolation, pixel-wise classification, and object-level majority voting. By adapting advanced hyperspectral transformers from earth observation and incorporating chemometric band selection, we achieve accurate classification of complex black plastics. The study establishes the first comprehensive benchmark using nine processing methods, including chemometric, machine learning, and DL architectures. To ensure reproducibility, the complete dataset and methodologies are publicly released, establishing a benchmark for a hyperspectral object-analysis pipeline in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [40] [Pixel-wise Geo-registration of Drone and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28891)
*Qingyang Liu,David G Shatwell,Parth Parag Kulkarni,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SkyReg数据集和基准，用于像素级无人机到卫星的地理配准，提供密集的每像素地理定位监督，评估多种基线方法，并训练出性能显著提升的几何感知重建流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多仅提供单个坐标标签，限制了对图像整体密集地理对齐的评估，因此需要更全面的数据集和评估标准来推动像素级跨视图地理注册的发展。

Method: 构建包含多种视角、场景类型和相机配置的SkyReg数据集，设计标准化评估基准，评估检索、特征匹配、单应性对齐及前馈3D重建等多种方法，并基于该数据集训练几何感知重建模型。

Result: 所提出的几何感知重建管道在多个指标上达到当前最优性能，显著提升了无人机与卫星图像之间的像素级地理对齐精度。

Conclusion: SkyReg为像素级跨视图地理注册提供了重要的数据资源和评估基准，推动了高精度地理对齐技术的发展，为未来研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Pixel-level cross-view geo-registration aims to align a query image (e.g., drone) to a geo-referenced satellite map so that every query pixel can be mapped to real-world GPS coordinates. Despite strong progress in cross-view geo-localization, existing benchmarks largely provide only GPS labels, limiting evaluation to a single coordinate per image and leaving dense geodetic alignment underexplored. We introduce SkyReg, a dataset and standardized benchmark for pixel-level drone-to-satellite geo-registration, providing dense per-pixel geo-location supervision across diverse settings (orthographic and perspective), scene types (urban, landmark-centric, suburban/rural), and camera configurations. Using SkyReg, we evaluate a broad set of baselines spanning retrieval, feature matching, homography-based alignment, and feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Finally, cross-view pairs from SkyReg, we train a geometry-aware reconstruction pipeline that achieves state-of-the-art results,improving performance by a significant margin.

</details>


### [41] [ReconSplat: Generalizable 3D Scene Reconstruction Beyond Observed Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28895)
*Giuseppe Stracquadanio,Kevin Raj,Julia Grabinski,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReconSplat 是一种前馈式 3D 场景重建模型，旨在解决视图生成真实性与几何一致性之间的长期权衡问题。它基于 3D 高斯 splatting（3DGS）作为可微分的中间场景表示，并结合多视图潜在扩散模型（MV-LDM），实现外观和场景几何的联合精炼与补全。通过利用 3DGS 编码的变分 3D 潜在特征引导扩散过程，确保几何一致性。该方法在 RealEstate10K 和 DL3DV-10K 等真实世界基准上生成逼真的新视角图像和精确的深度图，在挑战性外推设置中优于现有方法，能够同时实现未见视角的合理外推与一致精确的场景几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景重建方法在生成未观测区域的逼真视图与保持几何一致性之间存在权衡。如何在不牺牲几何精度的前提下，实现高质量的外观生成与复杂场景的外推，是当前研究的关键挑战。

Method: ReconSplat 基于 3D 高斯 splatting（3DGS）构建可微分的中间场景表示，结合多视图潜在扩散模型（MV-LDM），通过将 3DGS 的变分 3D 潜在特征投影至 2D 潜在空间来引导扩散过程，从而实现外观与几何的联合优化。该方法在前馈框架下完成端到端训练，无需迭代优化。

Result: ReconSplat 在 RealEstate10K 与 DL3DV-10K 基准上均表现出色，生成的新型视图具有高度逼真性，深度估计准确，尤其在复杂外推任务中显著优于现有方法。其能够在保持几何一致性的同时，实现对未见视角的合理外推。

Conclusion: ReconSplat 成功实现了高保真视图生成与几何一致性的统一，为 3D 场景重建提供了高效、可扩展的前馈解决方案，特别适用于需要精确几何建模和复杂外推的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce ReconSplat, a feed-forward model for 3D scene reconstruction that aims to address the longstanding trade-off between plausible view generation for unobserved regions and geometric consistency, providing both geometrically aligned novel views and sharp depth estimates. Our approach builds on 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) as an intermediate differentiable scene representation and integrates it with a multi-view latent diffusion model (MV-LDM) trained to act simultaneously as a refiner and an inpainter for appearance and scene geometry. We enforce geometric consistency by guiding the diffusion process with variational 3D latent features for appearance and geometry, encoded by the feed-forward 3DGS representation and rasterized to 2D latent space. ReconSplat produces both photorealistic novel views and accurate depth maps on real-world benchmarks, RealEstate10K and DL3DV-10K, outperforming existing methods in challenging extrapolation setups. Notably, ReconSplat allows the extrapolation of unseen and challenging viewpoints jointly with coherent and precise scene geometry.

</details>


### [42] [Coarse to Fine: Iterative Adversarial Neural Cellular Automata for Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28909)
*Anh Thi Luu,Nick Lemke,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StyleGANCA 是首个基于 NCA 的轻量级通用生成对抗网络，结合了 StyleGAN 的映射网络与自适应风格调制，通过多尺度 NCA 合成过程实现潜在空间控制的图像生成。在 BloodMNIST 和 PathMNIST 数据集上，其参数量仅 617k，却达到最佳 FID 与 KID 分数，且生成图像保留类别信息，可有效支持多分类器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决医学影像数据共享中的隐私与法律限制问题，同时应对现有生成模型计算成本高、难以在资源受限设备上部署的挑战。

Method: 将 StyleGAN 的映射网络与自适应风格调制引入多尺度 NCA 合成流程，通过迭代局部交互实现潜在空间控制的图像生成，构建轻量级生成模型。

Result: 在 PathMNIST 上取得最优的 FID 与 KID 分数，仅使用 617k 参数，显著减少模型规模；生成图像保持类间差异，可有效用于下游分类任务训练。

Conclusion: StyleGANCA 在保证生成图像质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，为医疗影像合成提供高效、隐私友好的解决方案，具备在资源受限环境下的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large-scale, publicly available datasets have driven advances in deep learning, but privacy and legal restrictions often limit data sharing in medical imaging. Synthetic data generation offers a privacy-friendly alternative to enable the training of high-performance models on health data. While most state-of-the-art generative models produce high-quality images, they remain computationally expensive, which limits their applicability on resource-constrained hardware. We propose StyleGANCA, the first lightweight general-purpose NCA-based generative adversarial network. The architecture integrates a StyleGAN-inspired mapping network and adaptive style modulation into a multi-scale NCA synthesis process, enabling latent-controlled image generation through iterative local interactions. We evaluate StyleGANCA on BloodMNIST and PathMNIST against adversarial, variational, diffusion, and NCA-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that StyleGANCA achieves competitive image quality with substantially fewer parameters than baseline architectures, achieving the best FID and KID scores on PathMNIST with only 617k parameters. Furthermore, downstream experiments show that the generated images preserve class-specific information and effectively support the training of multi-class classifiers. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MECLabTUDA/StyleGANCA

</details>


### [43] [mmIR: Frequency-Space Inverse Rendering for 3D Millimeter-Wave Radar ADC Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28913)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出mmIR，一个开源的可微分FMCW雷达逆渲染框架，通过物理模型拟合真实数据并从密集虚拟天线阵列中重渲染，以合成高分辨率3D雷达数据。利用LiDAR提供的网格作为几何骨架，优化材质、法向和天线波束模式，实现相位一致的多输入多输出（MIMO）前向模型，支持多路径传播、极化与自由空间衍射。在7个室外和6个室内场景中，相比Sionna-RT，range-azimuth图的平均皮尔逊相关系数达0.914（对比0.307），且无需重新训练即可迁移至同位置单芯片雷达（相关系数0.554）。100×100元素的密集虚拟阵列生成的单帧3D占用图经LiDAR验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毫米波雷达数据分辨率低，硬件扩展成本高，合成方法受限于数据稀缺。需要一种能从有限实测数据中生成高质量高分辨率3D雷达数据的方法，以支持下游任务如感知与建模。

Method: 采用基于物理的可微分前向模型，结合LiDAR提供的几何网格作为先验，通过端到端自动微分优化每顶点的ITU材料属性、法向量及天线波束模式，模拟多径传播、极化效应与自由空间衍射，实现高保真雷达信号逆渲染。

Result: 在多个室外与室内场景中，生成的range-azimuth图与真实数据的皮尔逊相关系数达到0.914，显著优于Sionna-RT的0.307；跨设备迁移能力良好，无需再训练即在单芯片雷达上获得0.554相关性；100×100虚拟阵列生成的3D占用图经LiDAR验证准确。

Conclusion: mmIR成功实现了基于物理模型的高分辨率3D雷达数据合成，克服了真实数据稀疏与硬件限制问题，为雷达感知研究提供了强大工具，具备良好的泛化与可扩展性。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D radar data is scarce. Commodity mmWave sensors use small antenna arrays that limit angular resolution to several degrees, and existing datasets provide only 2D range-azimuth maps or sparse point clouds rather than raw analog-to-digital converter (ADC) signals. Hardware scaling is expensive, synthetic-aperture scanning is impractical at fleet scale, and learned synthesis methods are bottlenecked by the very data shortage they aim to address. We present mmIR, an open-source differentiable frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar inverse renderer that fits a physics-based forward model to real captures and re-renders from dense virtual apertures to synthesize high-resolution 3D radar data. Because radar resolution is too coarse to recover geometry directly, mmIR performs LiDAR-assisted inverse rendering: using LiDAR-derived meshes as a geometric scaffold, mmIR optimizes per-vertex International Telecommunication Union (ITU) physics materials, vertex normals, and antenna beam patterns through end-to-end automatic differentiation of a phase-coherent multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) forward model with multi-bounce propagation, polarization, and free-space diffraction. On seven outdoor and six indoor ColoRadar scenes, mmIR achieves 0.914 mean Pearson correlation on range-azimuth maps versus 0.307 for Sionna-RT. Scenes trained on a cascaded imaging radar transfer to a co-located single-chip radar without re-training (0.554 correlation), and dense virtual arrays (100x100 elements) produce single-frame 3D occupancy validated against LiDAR. Project page: https://mmwave-inverse-rendering.github.io/

</details>

### [44] [NLP-Driven Knowledge Extraction and Thematic Classification of Translated Ancient Indian Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28608)
*M. S. Rajeevan,B. Mini Devi,V. S. Anoop,C. Mallikarjuna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用NLP技术（如命名实体识别、BERTopic主题建模和Neo4j知识图谱）对古代印度医学文献《苏胥鲁塔本集》进行信息提取与结构化分析，实现疾病、疗法、药物等概念的自动识别与可视化，推动传统医学知识向现代数据驱动研究的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代印度医学文献内容丰富但语言复杂、格式古老，难以被现代研究者系统理解与利用，亟需借助计算方法实现知识的可访问性与组织化。

Method: 结合命名实体识别（NER）、BERTopic主题建模和Neo4j图数据库构建知识图谱，对翻译后的文本进行实体识别、主题分类与语义关系可视化。

Result: 成功实现了对古籍中医学概念的结构化提取与关联分析，验证了图数据库与主题模型在历史医学知识数字化中的有效性，提升了古医典的可读性与可检索性。

Conclusion: 该方法为古代医学知识的数字化与跨学科研究提供了可行路径，有助于推动医学信息学与数字人文的发展，使传统阿育吠陀智慧更易被当代科学界理解和应用。

Abstract: Ancient Indian medical texts like Sushruta Samhita have extensive information on diseases, treatments, and surgical techniques. Yet, their ancient format and use of intricate vocabulary pose difficulties in accessibility and systematic ordering. The research here utilizes Natural Language Processing (NLP) methods like Named Entity Recognition (NER), BERTopic modeling, and Knowledge Graph development in Neo4j to extract, categorize, and visualize important concepts based on translated versions. Thematic classification with BERTopic allows for the identification of the underlying medical topics, whereas NER supports the structured entity recognition of diseases, treatments, researchers, and medicinal plants. Graphbased network analysis with Neo4j also allows for the semantic representation of relationship among extracted entities, supporting knowledge retrieval and digital preservation. The findings illustrate how graph databases, topic modeling, and entity recognition facilitate the computational organization of Ayurveda's historical medical wisdom, closing the gap between the conventional texts and contemporary data-driven inquiry. The suggested method promotes historical text analysis, medical informatics, and digital humanities to make ancient Indian medical wisdom more accessible and understandable.

</details>


### [45] [Parametric Multimodal User Memory: Storing What Captions Cannot Carry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28609)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将感知记忆嵌入模型的个性化代理记忆系统，通过视觉-语言模型（VLM）定位上下文中的指代对象（什么和哪里），并结合专用编码器提取身份密钥（谁），以一次性令牌形式存储在注意力机制中，实现无需外部查询的高效记忆读取。实验表明，单独使用任一模块效果有限，但两者结合可达到接近0.96的召回率，显著优于仅依赖文本的检索方法。该系统在多模态任务中表现出色，且识别核心无需训练，注册成本极低。研究发现，感知身份应存储于参数化记忆库，而具体事实则适合存于文本存储中，二者协同使代理能同时记住用户所说的话及其特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化代理主要依赖文本形式的用户记忆（如对话转录），难以捕捉无法用文字描述的感知信息（如声音、外貌变化等），导致对用户整体认知不完整。本文旨在弥补这一缺失，构建一个能持久保存用户感知特征的记忆系统。

Method: 将感知记忆分解为两个子问题：1）利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）在上下文中定位指代对象（即‘什么’和‘哪里’）；2）使用专用编码器提取用户的身份密钥（即‘谁’），并将该密钥以单个内联标记形式存储于注意力机制中，在生成时直接读取。整个识别核心无需训练，支持快速注册。

Result: 在五种模态测试中，纯文本检索的召回率最低仅为0.11，远低于专用编码器的0.81；而联合使用VLM与编码器后，正确区域的召回率达到0.96，且能泛化至多说话人音频与视频场景。在PerceptMem数据集上，感知身份的记忆能力受参数容量限制，而具体事实受限于存储容量，验证了分层记忆设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 感知记忆应嵌入模型内部，通过参数化记忆库存储身份特征，而具体事实则保留在文本存储中。两者的组合使得智能代理不仅能记住用户说了什么，还能理解用户是谁，从而实现更全面、更个性化的交互能力。

Abstract: A personalized agent needs a user memory: a persistent model of who its user is. Today it is almost always text -- transcripts and captions retrieved by similarity. This serves the captionable half of a person ("my cat is named Bibi"), but discards the perceptual half no caption can hold: how a voice sounds, how a face reads across age and lighting, how tired someone sounds. We measure this loss across five modalities: a strong caption-based re-identifier recovers as little as 0.11 of a dedicated encoder's recall, collapsing toward chance on non-nameable signals.
  We instead ground perceptual memory in the model, decomposing recall into two subproblems: a vision-language model grounds the referent in context (what and where), and a dedicated encoder extracts an identity key (who), stored as one inline token read by attention at generation with no external round-trip. Neither suffices alone -- the VLM identifies cross-age faces at only 0.54 recall where a face encoder reaches 0.81, and an ungrounded encoder recognizes a two-person-scene referent at 0.05 -- yet together they reach correct-region oracle (0.96), generalizing to multi-speaker audio and video. The recognition core is training-free: it reproduces the encoder's recall on any frozen model at O(1) registration cost. On PerceptMem (12 domains, 1,080 tasks) perceptual identity is capacity-limited while exact facts are binding-limited: identity belongs in a parametric bank, facts in a text store. The two memories compose cleanly: an agent with both can remember not only what its user said, but also what they are like.

</details>


### [46] [Gurukul AI: An Interactive AI-Driven Educational Platform for Indian Education System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28611)
*Isha Narang,Sneh Gosai,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对大型语言模型（LLM）在印度教育场景中的适配性问题，构建了一个基于印度国家课程委员会（NCERT）教材的、与课程大纲对齐的问答数据集（Gurukul），涵盖9-12年级五门学科共18,720个问答对。通过在LLaMA 3.1 8B模型上进行微调，并结合检索增强生成（RAG）框架，开发了名为GurukulAI的开源平台，支持学生以英语和印地语提问、获取上下文化答案及练习考试题型。该工作实现了全球LLM能力与本土教育需求的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型主要基于西方数据训练，难以适配印度多元语言、应试导向且结构化的教育体系。当前缺乏与印度课程标准对齐的高质量教育数据集和工具，导致本地化教育应用受限。

Method: 1. 基于NCERT教材构建覆盖5个学科的课程对齐QA数据集；2. 使用该数据集对LLaMA 3.1 8B模型进行微调；3. 构建面向教育场景的检索增强生成（RAG）系统；4. 开发开源平台GurukulAI，支持多语言交互与个性化学习支持。

Result: 成功构建包含18,720个问答对的Gurukul数据集，实现对本地课程内容的精准覆盖；微调后的模型在教育任务中表现良好；GurukulAI平台上线并支持多语言交互，已为印度学生提供实际学习支持。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建本土化教育数据集与定制化AI系统，有效弥合了全球大模型能力与印度区域教育需求之间的鸿沟，为发展具有文化敏感性和教学适应性的智能教育工具提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) like ChatGPT and LLaMA have transformed AI-driven education, but these systems are predominantly trained on Western-centric data, making them ill-suited for regional curricula like India's. The Indian education system is linguistically diverse, exam-oriented, and structured around standardized syllabi, not addressed by existing datasets or tools. In this work, we curate a syllabus-aligned QA dataset based on NCERT (National Council of Educational Research and Training) textbooks for classes 9-12, capturing the content, context, and teaching style of Indian curricula. The final dataset, comprising 18,720 question-answer pairs across five subjects, is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingoIITGN/Gurukul. We fine-tune the LLaMA 3.1 8B model using this dataset and deploy it in a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework tailored to educational needs. We introduce GurukulAI, an open-access platform that enables Indian students to chat with the model, get doubts cleared, practice exam-style questions, receive contextual answers, and interact in both English and Hindi. By localizing AI for Indian classrooms, our work bridges the gap between global LLM capabilities and regional educational demands. The code is available at https://github.com/lingo-iitgn/GurukulAI.

</details>


### [47] [STAGEET: Stage-wise Typed Edit Tagging for Grammatical Error Correction with Arabic as a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28614)
*Wenjie Lou,Alaa Mamdouh Akef*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAGEET 是一种分阶段的类型化编辑标注框架，将序列到编辑（Seq2Edit）的监督信号重新组织为带有类型信息的可执行阶段，通过多阶段修正提升语法错误纠正（GEC）的可解释性。该方法将纠正过程分解为一系列中粒度的、具有特定类型的阶段，每个阶段使用独立的标签空间进行预测并生成中间句子，传递给下一阶段。实验表明，该方法在保持高效编辑型GEC性能的同时，显著增强了纠错轨迹的可观察性，并在QALB-2014上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列到编辑方法虽高效且局部可解释，但其单一编辑词汇无法清晰揭示纠错类型，限制了对修正过程的理解。因此，需要一种能够显式表达纠错类别与顺序的更可解释的框架。

Method: 提出STAGEET框架，将纠错任务分解为多个有序的中粒度类型化阶段；每个阶段独立预测对应类型的编辑操作，基于当前中间句子进行一次重写，并将结果传给下一阶段；实现方式包括共享编码器多头模型与阶段专用适配器，以及完全独立的阶段标记器。

Result: 在QALB-2014和ZAEBUC数据集上，STAGEET保持了竞争性的编辑型GEC性能，同时显著提升了纠错过程的可解释性；在QALB-2014上达到当前最优表现。

Conclusion: STAGEET通过引入类型化、阶段化的编辑标注机制，有效增强了语法错误纠正任务的可解释性，同时维持甚至提升性能，为构建透明、可审计的GEC系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sequence-to-edit approaches make grammatical error correction (GEC) efficient and locally interpretable by predicting edit labels over the input rather than generating a full corrected sentence. Their interpretability, however, is primarily operational: a label specifies how the string should change, but a single edit vocabulary does not always reveal the type of correction being made. We propose STAGEET, a stage-wise typed edit-tagging framework that reorganizes Seq2Edit supervision into typed executable stages and extends edit operations to correction categories. STAGEET decomposes correction into an ordered sequence of medium-grained typed stages; each stage predicts from its own label space, rewrites the current hypothesis once, and passes the resulting intermediate sentence to the next stage. We instantiate the framework as both an end-to-end shared-encoder multi-head model with stage-specific adapters and a fully specialized variant with one independent tagger per stage. Experiments on QALB-2014 and ZAEBUC show that category-aware staged correction retains competitive edit-based GEC performance while exposing a more inspectable correction trajectory, and attains state-of-the-art results on QALB-2014.

</details>


### [48] [From GenAI Virtual Patient Dialogue Logs to Teacher-Interpretable Process Evidence: A Learning Analytics Study in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28619)
*Xinyu Li,Zijian Li,Mengyu Xia,Luzhen Tang,Naping Chen,Changmin Lin,Danijela Gasevic,Dragan Gasevic,Yizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了生成式AI虚拟患者（GenAI VP）对话记录的编码分析是否能为临床推理过程提供可解释的教育证据。通过对210名二年级医学生在五周内完成的1,030次胸痛病例历史采集对话进行分析，采用行为频率、基于认知网络分析的局部共现以及基于转移网络分析的序列转移三层分析方法，发现高评分咨询不仅涉及更多历史采集活动，更体现在信息收集与症状探索之间与沟通、核查、组织和整合等行为的紧密关联；总结与组织行为更常引导后续验证或机制导向的追问。该研究展示了多层分析在揭示高质量历史采集过程模式方面的潜力，并支持医学教育中以过程为导向的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI虚拟患者（GenAI VP）虽能提供可重复的历史采集练习并保留完整的对话记录，但这些原始日志对教师而言难以直接使用：完整转录稿过于冗长，而最终评分又无法反映学习者是否有效跟进患者线索、检查不确定性或运用总结引导后续提问。因此，亟需一种能够提取对话过程特征的方法，以支持教师理解学习者的临床推理过程并提供针对性反馈。

Method: 本研究采用三层分析方法对编码后的GenAI VP对话数据进行分析：(1) 行为频率分析，统计各类对话行为的发生频次；(2) 局部共现分析（基于认知网络分析，ENA），识别不同行为之间的关联模式；(3) 序列转移分析（基于转移网络分析，TNA），考察行为之间的动态转换关系。数据来源于210名医学生在五周内完成的1,030次胸痛病例历史采集对话，每份对话由教师根据量表评分，并按每周中位数分为高分组与低分组。

Result: 高评分对话中，学习者表现出更高的历史采集活动频率，但差异并非仅由数量决定。关键在于行为间的连接方式：高评分对话中，信息收集与症状探索更频繁地与沟通、核查、组织和综合等行为共现。此外，总结与组织类行为之后更常出现验证性或机制导向的后续提问，表明其具有引导性作用。三层次分析共同揭示出高评分对话中的过程特征，体现出更系统、反思性和以患者为中心的推理风格。

Conclusion: 通过多层次分析，编码后的GenAI VP对话日志能够有效揭示历史采集中的临床推理过程模式。该方法不仅能识别表现差异，还能为教师提供可解释的过程证据，从而支持医学教育中面向过程的反馈，提升教学效率与质量。

Abstract: Medical history taking is a dialogue-based clinical reasoning task in which learners must gather, organise, and integrate patient information while the consultation unfolds. Generative AI-powered virtual patients (GenAI VPs) make repeated history taking practice scalable and preserve full turn by turn dialogue. However, these logs are educationally difficult to use directly. Complete transcripts are too detailed for routine teacher review, whereas final scores obscure whether learners followed up patient cues, checked uncertainty, or used summaries to guide later questioning. This study examined whether coded GenAI VP dialogues can provide teacher-interpretable process evidence of clinical reasoning. We analysed 1{,}030 GenAI VP dialogues from 210 second-year medical learners across five weeks chest-pain cases. Each consultation was teacher-scored using a rubric assessing the full history taking dialogue, and consultations were classified within each week as high- or low-rated using the weekly median score. To explain how rated performance was reflected in the dialogue process, we applied three analytic layers to the same coded dialogue data: behavioural prevalence, local co-occurrence using Epistemic Network Analysis, and sequential transition using Transition Network Analysis. High-rated consultations involved more history taking activity, but differences were not simply about volume. High rated consultations more often connected information gathering and symptom exploration with communication, checking, organisation, and synthesis. Summarising and organising moves more often led to verification or mechanism-oriented follow-up. These findings show how layered analysis of GenAI VP dialogue logs can reveal process patterns associated with high rated history taking and support process-focused feedback in medical education.

</details>


### [49] [Looking Again: Measuring Sycophancy in the Reasoning Chains of Multimodal Models Under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28623)
*Mahir Numayeer Islam,Gakuto Okuyama,Nikolaus Siauw,Shivank Garg,Madhur Panwar,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估大型多模态推理模型（LMRMs）在面对用户错误答案时的迎合倾向（sycophancy）的基准和数据集。通过四个视觉基础数据集（涵盖数学、临床、时间及人口统计推理）与五种压力条件，在单轮和多轮对话设置下进行测试，发现压力环境下迎合现象普遍存在，尤其在陈述性压力下最为显著。多轮对话中临床视觉判断任务的推理阶段迎合率高达95.7%。研究还构建了推理链与最终答案层面的失败分类体系，并定位到迎合行为首次出现的具体语句层级，表明仅评估最终答案不足以捕捉其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型多模态推理模型虽依赖显式思维链提升性能，但缺乏可靠方法衡量其在面对用户错误输入时的迎合倾向。这种倾向可能导致模型违背证据而盲目顺从用户，影响推理可靠性。因此亟需建立系统性的评估框架以揭示并分析该问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含四种视觉基础任务的数据集，结合五种压力情境（如陈述压力、信念强度等），在单轮与多轮交互设置下测试模型表现；采用回答级与推理链级双重评估方式，引入失败分类体系区分不同层次的迎合行为，并通过句子级分析定位迎合行为首次出现的位置。

Result: 实验显示，在压力条件下，几乎所有模型均表现出显著的迎合倾向，其中陈述压力引发最高比例的迎合行为，而信念强度压力最低。多轮交互中，临床视觉判断任务的推理阶段迎合率高达95.7%。研究发现，迎合可独立于最终答案发生于推理链早期阶段，说明仅考察输出结果会严重低估问题程度。

Conclusion: 迎合倾向是多模态推理模型中的普遍且严重问题，尤其在高压或多轮场景下加剧。必须同时关注推理链过程与最终答案，才能全面评估模型的鲁棒性。本研究提出的基准与分类体系为未来模型安全性和可信性研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Large multimodal reasoning models (LMRMs) are getting increasingly capable, primarily through generating explicit chain-of-thought reasoning before answering. In language models it has been observed that this performance often comes with sycophancy, the tendency of a model to agree with the user over the evidence. However, for LMRMs no reliable method to measure sycophancy yet exists. We bridge this gap by introducing a benchmark and dataset for evaluating LMRM sycophancy when confronted with a wrong answer from a user. Our benchmark pairs four visually grounded datasets spanning mathematical, clinical, temporal, and demographic reasoning with five pressure conditions in single-turn and multi-turn settings. We evaluate sycophancy in the final answer as well as its emergence within the reasoning chain. We find that sycophancy is prevalent under pressure, with Statement pressure eliciting the highest rates and Conviction the lowest for all models except Mistral-Small-4, and under multi-turn pressure reasoning-level sycophancy intensifies sharply in clinical visual judgement, reaching 95.7% for the most affected model. We further introduce a failure taxonomy separating reasoning-chain from answer-level sycophancy, and a complementary sentence-level taxonomy locating where in the chain drift first emerges. Our results show that sycophancy can corrupt the reasoning chain independently of the final answer, so answer-level evaluation alone is insufficient.

</details>


### [50] [MA-RAG: Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Query-Driven Summarization of Longitudinal Parkinson's Disease Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28624)
*Sana Alamgeera,Denise Goberta,Muhammad Irshad,Anne H. H. Ngu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MA-RAG框架，通过多智能体检索增强生成技术，实现帕金森病临床评估数据的准确、时序一致且临床可信的摘要生成，显著提升事实准确性与专家认可度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前帕金森病的单次及纵向临床评估解读耗时且依赖专业经验；大语言模型虽能生成自然语言摘要，但缺乏临床领域知识支撑，难以保证事实正确性和时间一致性。

Method: 设计了一种查询驱动的多智能体检索增强生成框架（MA-RAG），将临床推理分解为领域专用智能体，结合结构化事实提取，并通过最终验证阶段合成临床基础摘要，支持四种临床分析任务：单次会话、轨迹、比较和队列总结。

Result: 在客观指标上，相比传统方法、仅RAG和单智能体RAG基线，MA-RAG在事实精度上提升达122%（从0.436增至0.990），幻觉率降低98%（从0.564降至0.010）；主观评价中临床专家对组织性与临床实用性给予最高评分。

Conclusion: 领域专业化多智能体推理能够实现结构化纵向临床评估数据的可靠、查询驱动式摘要生成。

Abstract: Accurate interpretation of single-visit and longitudinal clinical assessments for Parkinson's disease is time-consuming and often depends on specialist expertise. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate natural language summaries, they frequently lack domain-specific clinical grounding and struggle to produce factually correct and temporally consistent responses for structured longitudinal assessment data. To address these limitations, we propose MA-RAG, a query-driven multi-agent retrieval-augmented generation framework that decomposes clinical reasoning into domain-specialized agents, combines structured fact extraction, and synthesizes clinically grounded summaries through a final verification stage. The framework supports four clinical analysis tasks: single-session, trajectory, comparison, and cohort summarization. We evaluate MA-RAG using objective metrics, namely Fact Precision, Hallucination Rate, Temporal Fidelity, and Semantic Similarity, together with subjective evaluations conducted by clinical experts. Compared to Traditional, RAG-only, and Single-agent RAG baselines, MA-RAG substantially improves factual correctness, achieving up to a 122% relative increase in Fact Precision (from 0.436 to 0.990) and reducing the Hallucination Rate by up to 98% (from 0.564 to 0.010), while consistently receiving top ratings from clinical experts for organization and clinical usefulness. These results demonstrate that domain-specialized multi-agent reasoning enables reliable query-driven summarization of structured longitudinal clinical assessment data.

</details>


### [51] [Asymmetric Within-Document Predictive Learning for Scientific Document Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28625)
*You Zuo,Éric de la Clergerie,Benoît Sagot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SciJEPA框架，通过无引文的文档内预测学习科学文献表示，利用标题、摘要、方法和结论间的不对称预测关系，结合Sliced Isotropic Gaussian Regularization（SIGReg）提升性能，在多个任务上表现良好，证明了文档内预测学习在科学文本表示中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文档表示方法依赖引文信息，但引文可能不完整或有偏差；因此需要一种无需引文的替代方法来学习文档内部结构。

Method: 设计一个基于文档内不同部分（如标题、摘要、方法、结论）之间不对称预测的框架，采用预测预训练方式，并引入SIGReg控制嵌入空间几何结构。

Result: 预测训练本身有效但弱于对比学习基线；加入SIGReg后显著提升性能，缩小差距；不同正则化强度对任务效果有差异，且不同编码分支适合不同检索场景。

Conclusion: 在嵌入几何结构被合理控制的前提下，文档内预测学习是一种有前景的无引文科学文档表示方法。

Abstract: We study predictive pretraining for scientific document representation using the discourse structure of papers. We propose SciJEPA, a citation-free framework that learns through asymmetric within-document prediction: title and abstract representations are used to predict method representations, and method representations are used to predict conclusion representations. Experiments on RELISH, high-influence citation, SciDocs, and cite prediction show that plain predictive training is viable but weaker than a controlled contrastive baseline using the same section pairs. Adding Sliced Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg) substantially improves performance and narrows this gap. The effect of regularization is task-dependent: moderate SIGReg helps fine-grained ranking, while stronger regularization can weaken local alignment. We further show that different encoding branches support different retrieval regimes. These results position within-document predictive learning as a promising citation-free complement for scientific document representation, provided that embedding geometry is carefully controlled.

</details>


### [52] [Do large language models scrutinise what they review? A multimodal audit of scoring calibration, error detection, and author-identity effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28626)
*Emad Alharbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了两种多模态大语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-72B 和 Pixtral-Large-124B）在国际学习表征会议2026投稿上的评审能力，发现其评分普遍高于人类评审（7.0–8.1 vs. 3.4–6.8），但对已知错误的识别率较低（自然提示下仅12.1%，验证提示下提升至22.2%），且图像信息未能有效辅助错误检测；作者身份不影响评分或检测表现；模型决策与简单平均分一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在学术同行评审中的批判性评估能力，尤其是在真实会议场景下的表现，以评估其作为评审工具的可行性与局限性。

Method: 将165篇论文提交给两个多模态LLM，采用盲审、高/低声誉机构替换作者身份、文本或图文格式，并插入145个可验证错误，通过自然提示和验证指令测试错误识别能力，分析评分、决策一致性及视觉信息利用情况。

Result: LLM评分显著高于人类，但错误检测率仍很低（最多22.2%），图像未提升检测效果，反而提高评分；多数文本评论虚构不存在的图；作者身份无影响；模型决策与简单平均分完全一致。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在评分上表现出高一致性与稳定性，但其在关键的错误识别与事实核查方面存在严重不足，尚无法独立承担同行评审任务，需谨慎应用于实际评审流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate peer reviews, prompting examination of their capacity for critical evaluation. This study evaluates two multimodal LLMs, Qwen2.5-VL-72B and Pixtral-Large-124B, as reviewers across 165 submissions to the 2026 International Conference on Learning Representations, a venue that postdates both models' training cutoffs. Manuscripts were presented to both models with author identities blinded, replaced with high-prestige affiliations, or replaced with low-prestige affiliations, and in either text-only or text-with-figure format. Additionally, 145 verifiably detectable errors were inserted into 55 manuscripts to assess error identification under natural and verification-oriented prompts. Across all manuscript groups, including rejected submissions, LLM scores ranged from 7.0 to 8.1, whereas human mean scores ranged from 3.4 to 6.8. The models detected 12.1\% of the verified errors under natural prompting, and a one-sentence verification instruction increased detection to 22.2\%; however, 78\% of the errors remained undetected. Providing figures reduced error detection while increasing review scores. No visual error was reliably verified against its corresponding figure, and half of the text-only reviews described figures that were not provided. Author identity did not influence either review scores or error detection. LLM editorial decisions exactly matched those produced by simple score averaging.

</details>


### [53] [Intelligent Identification and Repair of Design Defects in BIM via Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28629)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Yun-Hong Cai,Xiang-Rui Ni,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于领域特定大语言模型（LLM）的集成框架，用于高效识别和修复建筑信息模型（BIM）中的多样化设计缺陷。通过引入组件平衡分块的BIM-to-Text方法，实现BIM数据与LLM的有效对接；结合规则注入、少样本提示和检索增强生成（RAG），提升缺陷识别与修复建议生成能力；并设计了融合关键标识符验证与词元长度阈值的幻觉控制策略，显著提高结果可靠性。实验表明，该方法在缺陷识别准确率上达到85%（传统规则检查为70%），修复建议合理率达94%，且在单轮干预中消除92.5%的幻觉，准确率从64%提升至85%。研究构建了从原始BIM数据输入到修复建议输出的端到端原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏通用性，难以高效识别和解决BIM中多样化的设计缺陷，亟需一种可扩展、可靠且智能的解决方案。

Method: 提出BIM-to-Text方法（组件平衡分块）、结合规则注入、少样本提示与RAG的提示学习框架，以及基于关键标识符验证和词元长度阈值的幻觉控制策略，实现缺陷识别与修复建议生成的端到端流程。

Result: 缺陷识别准确率达到85%（传统方法70%），修复建议合理率94%；幻觉控制使准确率从64%提升至85%，单轮干预消除92.5%幻觉。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一个从原始BIM数据输入到修复建议输出的端到端智能框架，具备高准确性、强泛化能力和良好可靠性，为BIM质量保障提供了有效技术路径。

Abstract: Existing methods lack a generalized approach to efficiently identify and resolve the diversity of design defects in BIM. Therefore, this study proposes an integrated framework to identify and repair various defects in BIM via domain-specific LLMs. Firstly, a BIM-to-Text method with component-balanced chunking is introduced to bridge BIM data with LLMs. Then, prompt learning with rule injection, few-shot prompting and RAG is proposed to identify defects and generate repair suggestions. Meanwhile, a hallucination control strategy combining key identifier validation and token-length thresholds is introduced to ensure reliability. Experiments show capability expansion yields 85% identification accuracy versus 70% for traditional rule checking, achieving a 94% rate of reasonable repair suggestions. Moreover, the proposed hallucination control further increased accuracy from 64% to 85%, eliminating 92.5% of hallucinations in a single intervention round. This study establishes an end-to-end prototype from raw BIM data input, through defect identification, to repair suggestion generation.

</details>


### [54] [Enabling Proactive Spoken Turns via a Generalized Style-Aware Full-Duplex Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28630)
*Tianrui Pan,Qinglin Zhang,Chong Deng,Luyao Cheng,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Jie Tang,Gangshan Wu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的风格感知全双工对话框架，通过轻量级主动语音轮次控制器LPS-TC、大规模真实世界数据集WildTurn以及两级评估方案，实现了在不牺牲响应质量的前提下提升对话时机控制能力。该框架可无缝集成至半双工与全双工模型中，支持细粒度风格调控和强泛化性，显著提升了对话的自然度与人类交互体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工对话系统在实时主动交互中面临时机控制与响应质量之间的权衡问题，尤其在真实场景下缺乏对主动打断、回应对话等行为的有效建模，亟需更灵活且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出LPS-TC轻量级主动语音轮次控制器，构建包含2981小时真实多轮立体对话的WildTurn数据集，并设计分层级的评估体系，结合半双工与全双工模型进行验证。

Result: LPS-TC在多种模型上均展现出优越的时机适配性和响应质量；在真实流式约束下实现精准的轮次控制与高质量交互，具备细粒度风格可控性和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了全双工对话中主动交互的时机与质量平衡难题，推动了更自然、更接近人类交互的实时对话系统发展。

Abstract: Compared with half-duplex dialogue systems where the system waits for user turn completion before it responds, natural full-duplex dialogue systems require agents to act proactively in real time, including timely interruptions and backchannels. This creates a key challenge: improving turn timing without sacrificing response quality. To address limitations in realistic proactive turn-taking, we build a generalized style-aware full-duplex framework with three key components. Firstly, we propose LPS-TC, a Lightweight Proactive Speech Turn Controller for plug-and-play integration. It features a fine-grained action space covering both reactive and proactive turn behaviors, enabling half-duplex models with full-duplex capabilities and enhancing existing full-duplex models with superior timing control. Secondly, we construct WildTurn, a large-scale, real-world English dataset containing approximately 2,981 hours of filtered multi-turn stereo conversations from face-to-face and telephone conversations, annotated with five turn-taking and five backchanneling styles. Trained on WildTurn, LPS-TC exhibits rich spoken dynamics that are not captured by existing static full-duplex benchmarks. Thirdly, we introduce a two-tier evaluation scheme that assesses both chunk-level timing precision and turn-level interaction quality under realistic streaming constraints. Our experiments, integrating LPS-TC with half-duplex models like Qwen2.5-Omni and full-duplex models like Freeze-Omni, showcase its superior performance in timing appropriateness and response quality. Our framework also demonstrates fine-grained style controllability and strong generalizability, enabling more natural and human-like spoken interactions.

</details>


### [55] [PAUSE: Editable Strategy Artifacts for Long-Form Cultural Story Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28633)
*Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Mohammad Saifullah,Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PAUSE是一种干预方法，通过暴露可编辑的文化适应策略作为人类控制界面，使生成式AI在长篇故事改编中的文化决策更透明、可修改。在两部中文源连载小说中测试表明，人类对策略的编辑能有效传递到章节级文本，9个对比案例中所有评委均偏好编辑后的输出，且目标标记在8/9的编辑输出中出现，而对照组为0；禁止标记在编辑输出中未出现，而在对照组中普遍存在。研究将结果视为一次验证编辑遵循性的‘烟雾检测’实验，而非宣称输出具有文化权威性或文学质量提升。PAUSE提供了一种使AI文化适应更具可审查性和可争议性的实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在文化适应中扮演重要角色，但其文化决策常隐藏于提示、模型计划或最终文本中，缺乏透明度和可操作性。需要一种机制使文化决策可被人类检查、修改并追踪影响。

Method: 提出PAUSE（Pause-And-Update Strategy Editing）方法，将文化适应策略构建为结构化可编辑实体，并在故事生成流程中分阶段应用该策略，以检验其是否能有效影响下游章节内容。通过双源中文小说进行对照实验，比较编辑策略与未编辑策略生成的章节文本。

Result: 在9组对比中，评委一致选择编辑策略生成的内容；标记审计显示，目标标记出现在8/9的编辑输出中，而对照组为0；禁止标记在编辑输出中缺失，但在所有对照组中出现。

Conclusion: PAUSE成功实现了文化适应策略的可编辑性与可追踪性，使生成过程更具透明度和可控性，是提升AI文化适应可审查性和可争议性的有效手段。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly mediate cultural adaptation, but their cultural decisions are often hidden inside prompts, transient model plans, or final prose. We study PAUSE (Pause-And-Update Strategy Editing), an intervention that exposes an editable adaptation strategy as a human control surface for cultural decisions in long-form story adaptation. The strategy is a structured artifact that can be inspected, edited, and then projected through downstream character, entity, and chapter-localization stages. In two Chinese-source serialized novels, we test whether human edits to this strategy propagate into chapter-level prose. Across 9 edited-vs-control chapter comparisons, judges select the edited-strategy output in all 9; a marker audit shows target markers in 8/9 edited outputs and 0/9 controls, with forbidden markers absent from edited outputs and present in all controls. We frame these results as a smoke-scale edit-adherence study, not a claim that the outputs are culturally authoritative or literary-quality improvements. PAUSE offers one practical way to make AI-mediated cultural adaptation more inspectable and contestable before decisions propagate through long-form generation.

</details>


### [56] [Do MLLMs Really Understand Low-Resource Khmer Documents? A Pilot Study on Khmer Document VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28635)
*Nimol Thuon,Panhapin Theang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对开放的多模态大模型（MLLMs）在高棉文文档图像上的表现进行了初步诊断评估，基于KH-FUNSD数据集构建了包含发票、收据、报价单等业务表单的评估子集，涵盖英、高棉语及混合脚本的问题与答案。实验比较了直接图像提示、解析器辅助和外部OCR辅助配置下的Qwen3-VL-8B等模型性能。结果显示，直接使用Qwen3-VL-8B达到51.9%整体准确率，而外部OCR（Tesseract与PaddleOCR）分别达61.9%和61.6%，但高棉文脚本答案仍显著困难。当前模型可较好处理清晰英文和结构化数值内容，但对原生高棉文文档理解仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在低资源、非拉丁语系场景下的可靠性尚未明确，尤其是高棉文文档因复杂脚本、中英混合字段及双货币值而具有特殊挑战，且相关资源匮乏。因此需评估当前模型在高棉文文档理解中的实际能力与局限。

Method: 从KH-FUNSD数据集中构建评估子集，覆盖多种业务表单；设计包含英、高棉语及混合脚本问题的测试集；采用直接图像提示、解析器辅助与外部OCR辅助三种方式评估Qwen-VL系列模型，重点分析不同答案形式的表现差异。

Result: 直接使用Qwen3-VL-8B获得51.9%的整体准确率，外部OCR辅助下分别达到61.9%（Tesseract）和61.6%（PaddleOCR），但高棉文脚本答案的识别性能远低于英文与数值字段，表明模型对原生高棉文理解仍不充分。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型能够有效处理视觉清晰的英文和结构化数值内容，但在高棉文文档理解方面仍面临显著挑战，尤其在纯高棉文与混合脚本答案上表现不佳，说明可靠地支持原生高棉文文档理解仍是开放性难题。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced document understanding, visual question answering, and text extraction. However, their reliability in low-resource, non-Latin settings remains uncertain. Khmer form documents present particular challenges because they contain complex script forms, mixed Khmer-English fields, and monetary values in both Cambodian Riel and US Dollars. Available resources for Khmer Document VQA are also limited. This paper presents a pilot diagnostic evaluation of open MLLMs on Khmer document images. We construct an evaluation subset from the previously introduced KH-FUNSD collection, covering invoices, receipts, quotations, and other business forms. The subset includes questions in English and Khmer, with answers retained in their original English, Khmer, mixed-script, or numeric forms. Rather than introducing a full public benchmark, this study examines the capabilities and failure modes of existing models. We evaluate representative open Qwen-VL models using direct image-based prompting and compare parser-assisted and external OCR-assisted configurations with Qwen3-VL-8B. Direct Qwen3-VL-8B outperforms smaller models, achieving 51.9% overall accuracy, although performance remains limited for Khmer-script and mixed-script answers. External OCR produces the strongest results, reaching 61.9% with Tesseract and 61.6% with PaddleOCR. Nevertheless, Khmer-script answers remain substantially more difficult than English and numeric fields. The results indicate that current MLLMs can process visually clear English and structured numeric content, but reliable native Khmer document understanding remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [57] [PromptKWS: A Novel Prompt-Guided Open-Vocabulary Keyword Spotting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28640)
*Gaopeng Xu,Chengfei Li,Xianliang Wang,Lin Zhu,Juan Wei,Wenpeng Li,Jianwei Niu,Jie Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptKWS 是一种新型的提示引导关键词检测（KWS）框架，通过引入提示短语预测网络（PPN）和提示-声学多头交叉注意力机制，显著提升开放词汇KWS系统的准确性。实验表明，相比基线系统，其唤醒率提升超过10%，在噪声和发音变化等复杂环境中平均准确率提升超过15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇关键词检测系统在复杂环境（如噪声和发音差异）下性能下降，亟需更有效的方法来提升鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 提出PromptKWS框架，包含提示短语预测网络（PPN）用于提取关键词提示嵌入，并通过提示-声学多头交叉注意力（MHCA）将提示嵌入注入到KWS编码器中，增强模型对关键词的理解与适应能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，PromptKWS在唤醒率上比基线系统提升超过10%，在复杂真实环境下平均准确率提升超过15%，表现出优异的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: PromptKWS通过提示引导机制有效提升了开放词汇关键词检测系统的准确性和环境适应性，尤其在噪声和发音变异场景下表现突出，具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present PromptKWS, a novel Prompt-guided keyword spotting (KWS) framework to improve the accuracy of open vocabulary KWS systems. In specific terms, we introduce the Prompt Phrases Prediction Network (PPN), an encoder-decoder architecture designed to effectively extract keyword prompts embeddings. we employ the PPN encoder to encode the keyword prompts and infuse the prompt embedding into the Prompt-guided KWS encoder by utilizing a Prompt-acoustic Multi-head Cross-attention (MHCA). Experiments show that PromptKWS improves the wakeup rate by over 10% compared to baseline system. Notably, another strength of PromptKWS is its ability to effectively leverage keyword prompts for adapting to complex real-world environments involving noise and pronunciation variations. In comparison to purely acoustic models, which often struggle in such situations, PromptKWS demonstrates remarkable performance, with an average accuracy improvement of over 15% in test sets.

</details>


### [58] [Terminal-Bench-LILT: Multilingual Agentic Coding Benchmark Grounded in Language, Region, and Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28641)
*Yunsu Kim,Kaden Uhlig,Ashwin Purohit,Milind Agarwal,Patrick Simianer,Anil Arslan,Kiarash Mokhtari,Thomas Zenkel,Johannes Mosig,Gabriel Bretschner,Shamik Bose,Joern Wuebker,John DeNero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Terminal-Bench-LILT，一个包含300个真实编程任务的多语言基准测试集，覆盖阿拉伯语、捷克语、德语、西班牙语、印地语、日语、韩语、塞尔维亚语、土耳其语和中文等十种语言。这些任务聚焦非英语软件开发中的特有问题，如国际化、编码、文本归一化和文化惯例，且均由母语程序员编写并经过多阶段质量控制。对六个前沿模型的评估显示，最强模型仅达到63.1%的通过率，且许多任务未被任何模型解决。不同语言间性能差异显著，且不与通用编码基准排名一致，表明多语言编程能力是一个独立且尚未充分探索的能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数代码生成模型的评估仅限于英语环境，无法反映其在真实多语言场景下的表现。为填补这一空白，需要构建一个真实、多样且具有挑战性的多语言编程基准，以评估模型在非英语编程任务中的实际能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含300个真实编程任务的多语言数据集，涵盖十种语言；所有任务由母语程序员设计，涉及国际化的具体问题（如编码、文本归一化、文化规范等）；采用多阶段质量控制流程确保任务质量和多样性；使用该数据集对多个前沿代码生成模型进行评估。

Result: 评估结果显示，即使是最先进的模型在该多语言基准上的通过率也仅为63.1%，且大量任务未能被任何模型成功解决；不同语言间的性能差异明显，且不与传统英文编码基准的表现趋势一致，说明多语言编程能力是独立且亟待研究的能力维度。

Conclusion: 现有代码生成模型在多语言编程任务中表现有限，表明多语言编程能力是一个不同于通用编程能力的重要新维度，亟需进一步研究与提升。此基准测试集为未来多语言代码模型的发展提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Most evaluations for coding agents are conducted exclusively in English, which does not reflect real-world multilingual deployment. We present Terminal-Bench-LILT, a suite of 300 authentic coding tasks in ten languages: Arabic, Czech, German, Spanish, Hindi, Japanese, Korean, Serbian, Turkish, and Chinese. Each task targets issues specific to non-English software development that have no direct English equivalent, e.g., internationalization, encoding, text normalization, and cultural conventions. All tasks are authored by native-speaker programmers and validated through a multi-stage quality control pipeline. Evaluation of six frontier models reveals that even the strongest model reaches only 63.1\% pass rate, with many tasks unsolved by any model. Performance varies substantially by language and does not track general coding benchmark rankings, highlighting that multilingual coding competence is a distinct and underexplored capability axis. Sample tasks are available at https://github.com/lilt/terminal-bench-lilt

</details>


### [59] [Redesigning and Auditing Deep Research Writing for Faithful Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28643)
*Hiroaki Hayashi,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAIMPROBE，一种基于声明的审计方法，用于检测深度研究系统在生成报告时的细粒度事实错误。通过该方法发现，即使整体评分稳定，强模型仍可能遗漏关键证据或错误归因。为此，作者进一步提出CLAIMWRITER，一种分层的声明驱动写作框架，能从源信息中提取事实并映射到查询导出的结构化大纲，以带来源引用的声明形式撰写各部分。在三个现有深度研究框架上，仅替换报告生成器为CLAIMWRITER即可使幻觉减少2.6至4.5倍，必要事实召回率提升1.2至1.7倍，同时保持报告整体质量。此外，该方法支持局部更新，在源信息变更时能高效传播更新内容，且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分的评估方法无法揭示深度研究系统在生成报告时的细粒度事实错误，如幻觉、误归因和证据遗漏。尽管整体评分稳定，但实际生成质量可能存在严重缺陷，亟需更精细的评估与改进机制。

Method: 提出CLAIMPROBE，将报告分解为可验证的声明，评估幻觉、误归因、引文规范性和必要事实召回；设计CLAIMWRITER，采用分层结构，基于源事实和查询导出的结构化大纲，逐段生成带源引用的声明式内容。

Result: 使用CLAIMPROBE发现，现有强模型存在关键证据遗漏与误归因问题；引入CLAIMWRITER后，幻觉降低2.6至4.5倍，必要事实召回率提升1.2至1.7倍，报告质量基本维持不变；在源更新场景下，其局部修订效率最高且成本更低。

Conclusion: CLAIMPROBE有效揭示了深度研究系统中的隐性事实错误，而CLAIMWRITER通过声明驱动的结构化写作显著提升了生成报告的事实准确性与可维护性，是提升生成质量的有效方案。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluations of deep-research (DR) systems often obscure fine-grained factual failures in generated reports. We introduce CLAIMPROBE, a claim-level audit that decomposes DR reports into claims and measures hallucination, misattribution, citation hygiene, and necessary-fact recall against retrieved evidence. Using CLAIMPROBE, we find that strong DR pipelines can omit key evidence and misattribute claims even when their rubric scores remain stable. We then propose CLAIMWRITER, a hierarchical claim-based writer that extracts source facts, maps them to a query-derived outline, and drafts each section from a source-linked claim representation. Across three prior DR frameworks, replacing only the report writer with CLAIMWRITER reduces hallucination by 2.6 to 4.5 times and improves necessary-fact recall by 1.2 to 1.7 times, while largely preserving overall report quality. CLAIMWRITER also enables localized revision: when sources change, it propagates changed source facts into revised reports at the highest rate among update methods, while also being more cost-effective.

</details>


### [60] [Cross Lingual Transfer in Tulu Legal Comprehension: Script-Dependent Improvement and RAG-Induced Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28645)
*Sindhu Shetty,Spurthi Setty,Natan Vidra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在低资源语言环境下，模型通过跨语言迁移和检索增强生成（RAG）框架进行法律投诉分类的表现。以德拉威语系的低资源语言图鲁语为对象，研究发现：通过音译查询至相关语言（如卡纳达语），模型可获得初步理解，但性能高度依赖于脚本；使用卡纳达语法律文档进行RAG检索后，部分模型表现出微弱正向趋势，但存在事实替换和幻觉等推理失败问题。分析表明，模型的解析与推理能力是失败主因，而非语料库内容本身。研究还提出基于推理轨迹和统计诚实性的评估框架，具有更广泛的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在低资源语言中缺乏有效的评估手段，尤其在跨语言迁移和检索增强生成（RAG）场景下，模型常出现推理错误，亟需系统化评估机制以识别其失效原因。

Method: 采用三个大模型（Llama3、Hex-1、Sarvam）在图鲁语法律投诉上测试，通过音译跨脚本输入提升理解，并结合卡纳达语法律语料进行RAG检索；利用推理轨迹分析与统计诚实性框架评估模型表现。

Result: 音译可促进初步理解，但效果受脚本影响显著；RAG检索带来混合结果，部分模型有微弱提升，但普遍存在事实替换与幻觉问题；推理失败主要源于模型自身处理能力，非语料质量。

Conclusion: 在低资源多语言RAG环境中，模型的推理与信息解析能力是关键瓶颈。脚本依赖性与RAG鲁棒性密切相关，建议采用推理轨迹与统计诚实性框架作为通用评估工具。

Abstract: Low-resource languages without an adequate training corpus often use a related, higher-resource language as a scaffold for comprehension. Still, there is a need to develop rigorous evaluation methods to identify when models fail in cross lingual low-resource environments. Using the legal domain as a backdrop, three models (Llama3, Hex-1, Sarvam) were tested on the ability to classify legal complaints written in a low resource Dravidian language (Tulu). Transliterating queries across Dravidian scripts allowed models to gain a preliminary understanding of speakers' complaints without the use of wide scale training, though the level of comprehension was heavily script dependent (with Kannada - another relatively low-resource language - producing the strongest positive trend). Retrieving from a corpus of Kannada legal papers across a RAG framework caused mixed results. Some models had a weak positive trend in comprehension under certain conditions, but when models failed, it was often across two axes: fact substitution (fixating on specific passage excerpts that skewed reasoning) and confabulation (hallucination that had no basis in either query or corpus). Within low resource domains, results identify the model's parsing of information and subsequent reasoning as the source of reasoning failure, rather than corpus contents. Script-dependent comprehension and RAG robustness also seem to travel together. This is further supported by the reasoning-trace analysis and a statistical-honesty framework deployed - techniques that are more broadly applicable to low-resource multilingual RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [61] [Can Large Language Models Identify Meaningful Touchpoints in Conversion Attribution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28649)
*Jinqi Wu,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Yong Bai,Chao Yi,Han Zhu,Shuodian Yu,Lei Zhang,Sheng Chen,Chenghuan Hou,Jian Xu,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究电商推荐与在线广告中的转化归因触点选择问题，指出当前基于协同过滤的启发式方法无法准确捕捉用户感知的语义意图。通过人工标注发现现有规则遗漏大量隐含相关且语义相关的触点。研究系统评估了大语言模型（LLM）在识别这些隐藏关联方面的表现，发现LLM能有效发现大量隐含触点，但仍存在提升空间。同时分析了不同提示策略和基础模型对识别效果的影响，揭示其推理模式与有效性，为从机械规则匹配转向人类对齐的语义推理提供了新路径。此外，利用LLM生成的转化标签改进工业级点击率预估模型训练，在离线测试中取得显著性能提升，展示了LLM在转化归因中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转化归因方法依赖协同过滤启发式规则，难以反映用户实际的语义意图，导致大量隐含但语义相关的触点被忽略，亟需更贴近用户认知的识别机制。

Method: 通过人工标注揭示语义差距；系统评估大语言模型在识别隐含触点上的能力；对比不同提示策略与基础模型的表现；利用LLM生成的转化标签优化工业级CVR模型训练。

Result: LLM能够有效发现大量未被传统规则捕获的隐含相关触点，显著提升触点识别能力；不同提示策略和模型选择对性能有显著影响；基于LLM标注的转化标签可显著提升工业级CVR模型的离线性能。

Conclusion: 大语言模型为转化归因提供了从规则驱动向语义驱动转变的新范式，不仅提升了触点识别的准确性，还具备实际应用价值，推动了推荐与广告系统的智能化升级。

Abstract: Touchpoint selection in conversion attribution, namely identifying meaningful touchpoints contributing to conversions, is essential for e-commerce recommendation and online advertising. Current selection methods rely heavily on collaborative-filtering-based heuristics, which fail to align with user-perceived semantic intent. Through human annotation, we reveal a significant semantic gap: many implicitly-related, semantically relevant touchpoints remain undetected by existing rules. Therefore, we systematically evaluate the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) in identifying these hidden associations. Our evaluation shows that while LLMs effectively uncover a substantial portion of implicitly-related touchpoints, significant room for improvement remains in their selection performance. Furthermore, we analyze the impact of different prompting strategies and foundation model choices on identification performance, providing valuable insights into their reasoning patterns and effectiveness. These insights offer a new roadmap for transitioning conversion attribution from mechanical rule-matching to human-aligned semantic reasoning. Moreover, we leverage the LLM-attributed conversion labels for enhancing industrial CVR model training and achieve significant offline performance gains, showing the potential of LLMs in conversion attribution.

</details>


### [62] [Test-Time Scaling for Scientific Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28660)
*Haowei Lin,Hubert Lim,Xiangyu Wang,Letian Huang,Di He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了测试时扩展（TTS）在开放式方程发现任务中的应用，将大语言模型驱动的方程发现建模为统一的迭代搜索过程，发现搜索宽度是影响性能的主要计算分配参数，在固定预算下，合适的宽度选择能提升并行性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索测试时扩展（TTS）在开放式方程发现任务中的有效性，以提升大语言模型的推理能力，并理解计算资源分配对模型表现的影响。

Method: 将方程发现建模为一个统一的迭代搜索过程，整合最佳N、序列优化、树搜索和进化式方法，通过最小化并行控制器在固定计算预算下进行对比实验。

Result: 搜索宽度是主要的分配参数，其最优值随计算预算增加而上升；种群-分支比例和控制器选择影响较小；合适宽度可提高墙钟效率。

Conclusion: 在具备有效验证器的前提下，控制探索与利用是实现大语言模型方程发现可扩展性的关键。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model reasoning by allocating additional test-time compute, but prior work mainly studies closed-ended tasks such as math and coding. We study TTS for automated equation discovery, an open-ended setting where models search over candidate equations and rely on observed datapoints for feedback. We formulate LLM-driven equation discovery as an iterative search process that unifies Best-of-N, sequential refinement, tree search, and evolution-style methods under a common compute-allocation view. To isolate allocation effects from prompt engineering and other heuristics, we compare minimal parallel controllers under fixed budgets. On LLM-SRBench equation-discovery tasks, we find that search width is the dominant allocation parameter: the best width in our sweep generally increases with the compute budget, while the population--branching split and controller choice matter less. Appropriate width selection also improves wall-clock efficiency by increasing parallelism. These results suggest that, given an informative verifier, controlling exploration and exploitation is central to scaling LLM-based equation discovery.

</details>


### [63] [GreenBench: Benchmarking Energy Efficiency and Carbon Footprint of Open-Source LLM Inference on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28667)
*Rajeswari Kannan,Raj Firke,Shreya Bengle,Srushti Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出GreenBench基准框架，评估了5个开源LLM（3-9B参数）在Apple M4 Pro芯片上的能效、吞吐量和碳足迹，发现其单用户部署下每token能效比数据中心GPU高30-40倍，小模型（3-3.8B）比大模型（7-9B）能效更高，且通过帕累托分析识别出Qwen 2.5（7B）和Llama 3.2（3B）分别为精度-效率与延迟敏感应用的最优选择，并提供不同电网下的碳排放估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在推理阶段的环境影响日益受到关注，但现有绿色AI研究主要集中在数据中心GPU和嵌入式平台，缺乏对Apple Silicon统一内存架构下能效的系统性评估。

Method: 采用macOS powermetrics进行直接功耗测量，结合Ollama的纳秒级计时技术，对五种开源LLM在三种NLP任务上进行测试，分析包级与系统级能耗及碳足迹。

Result: M4 Pro在持续推理中仅消耗0.47W CPU+GPU包功耗，系统总功耗为8-12W，能效提升30-40倍；小模型较大型号有2.6-4.2倍更高的吞吐量和最高62%更低的每token能耗；Qwen 2.5（7B）在准确率与效率间取得最佳平衡，而Llama 3.2（3B）适用于低延迟场景。

Conclusion: Apple M4 Pro在本地部署LLM方面展现出显著的能效优势，小模型更适合高效推理，且可通过绿电优化降低碳足迹，为边缘侧绿色AI提供新范式。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) has raised concerns about their environmental impact during inference. While Green AI research has focused on datacenter GPUs and embedded platforms, the energy profile of LLM inference on Apple Silicon, with its unified memory architecture, remains unstudied. This paper presents GreenBench, a benchmarking framework that evaluates the energy efficiency, throughput, and carbon footprint of five open-source LLMs (3-9B parameters) across three NLP tasks on an Apple M4 Pro with 48 GB unified memory. Using macOS powermetrics for direct power measurement and Ollama's nanosecond-precision timing, we find that the M4 Pro draws only 0.47 W of CPU+GPU package power during sustained inference, with total system power of 8-12 W, achieving 30-40x better energy efficiency per token than datacenter GPUs in single-user deployment. Smaller models (3-3.8B) deliver 2.6-4.2x higher throughput and up to 62% less energy per token than larger models (7-9B). Pareto analysis identifies Qwen 2.5 (7B) as the optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off at 57% MMLU and 59 tokens/s, while Llama 3.2 (3B) suits latency-critical applications at 175 tokens/s. We provide per-token energy at package and system levels with CO2 estimates for India and US grids.

</details>


### [64] [ReVA: A Region-Aware Visual Assistant for Visually Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28707)
*Anoop Senthil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReVA 是一种针对视觉问答（VQA）中空间推理与细粒度视觉理解挑战的区域感知模型，通过融合冻结的 CLIP ViT-L/14 与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct LLM，利用双桥结构对图像和区域级特征进行对齐。该模型引入自动零样本边界框检测机制（基于 RAM++、spaCy、Grounding DINO），将图像级与区域级特征分别映射为图像令牌和区域令牌，并作为前缀输入大语言模型，从而提升对场景与局部细节的理解能力。在 VQAv2、MMBench、POPE、SEED-Bench 等基准上表现优异，尤其在 POPE 上达到 82.85% 的平均 F1，显著优于仅使用图像令牌的基线（81.14%），有效减少对象、属性与空间幻觉，增强事实性与视觉接地性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在需要精确空间推理和细粒度视觉理解的任务中仍存在对象、属性和空间幻觉问题，主要源于缺乏区域级和细粒度的视觉定位能力。因此亟需一种能够显式建模局部视觉证据的机制以提升模型的视觉接地性。

Method: 提出 ReVA 模型，采用冻结的 CLIP ViT-L/14 提取图像特征，通过双桥结构分别处理整体图像和区域级特征：图像桥将最后一层变压器块输出映射为图像令牌；区域桥从多个中间层提取丰富特征，对每个边界框生成 K 个区域令牌，保留早期纹理与后期对象线索。结合自动零样本边界框检测器（RAM++、spaCy、Grounding DINO）生成问题无关与问题相关的边界框。最终将图像令牌与区域令牌拼接为大语言模型的提示前缀，实现联合编码。

Result: 在 POPE 基准上取得 82.85% 的平均 F1，优于仅使用图像令牌的基线（81.14%）；在 VQAv2、MMBench、SEED-Bench 等任务中也表现出色，证明区域感知表示能有效降低幻觉，提升多模态大模型的事实准确性与视觉接地性。

Conclusion: ReVA 通过显式引入区域级视觉表示，显著增强了多模态大语言模型在复杂视觉问答任务中的精确性和可靠性，特别是在缓解对象与空间幻觉方面具有明显优势，为未来高精度视觉理解系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet they continue to struggle with questions requiring precise spatial reasoning and fine-grained visual understanding. These limitations often manifest as object, attribute, and spatial hallucinations, where models generate confident but visually unsupported responses due to insufficient region-level and fine-grained visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose ReVA, a region-aware VQA model that employs a frozen CLIP ViT-L/14 Vision Transformer (ViT) and a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct large language model (LLM) connected through a dual bridge that aligns both whole-image and region-level representations with the LLM's embedding space. The image bridge maps final transformer block features into image tokens. The region bridge maps cropped features from enriched intermediate features across ViT blocks so early texture and later object cues are more evident, into K region tokens for every bounding box. ReVA uses a detector stack that supplies automatic zero-shot bounding boxes that are both question-agnostic and question-dependent, using RAM++ (Recognize Anything Model), spaCy, and Grounding DINO. The image tokens and region tokens are concatenated as an LLM prompt prefix to jointly encode scene-level context and fine-grained regional evidence when answering questions. Evaluated on VQAv2, MMBench, POPE, and SEED-Bench, ReVA achieves 82.85% mean F1 on POPE, compared with 81.14% for an image-token baseline without region tokens. These results demonstrate that explicit region-aware visual representations reduce object hallucination and improve the factual grounding of MLLMs.

</details>


### [65] [Evaluating Multilingual Sentence Embeddings for Translation Error Detection:An English--Greek Contrastive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28776)
*Eleftherios Kalogeros,Athanasios Ntalakas,Manolis Gergatsoulis,Paschalis Nikolaou,Sotiria-Lito Alexaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种通用多语言句子嵌入模型（BGE-M3、Multilingual E5、Multilingual MPNet、LaBSE、Jina Embeddings v3）在识别英语-希腊语翻译中细微错误方面的能力，并与参考无COMETKiwi模型对比。使用包含1,850个样本的对比数据集，涵盖十类核心和五类探索性错误。结果表明，BGE-M3表现最佳（准确率89.30%），而COMETKiwi达到94.49%。嵌入模型对事实性和词汇语义错误更敏感，但对时态、代词指代等错误检测能力较弱；COMETKiwi在时态、指代和语义角色错误上表现更好，但在日期时间及数字错误上表现较差。整体显示嵌入模型与质量评估模型具有互补性，建议将其作为综合翻译评估框架的一部分而非独立指标使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言句子嵌入广泛用于跨语言语义相似性估计，但其对细粒度翻译错误的敏感性尚未充分理解。本研究旨在检验通用多语言嵌入模型是否能有效区分正确翻译与微小修改的错误翻译，以评估其在翻译质量评估中的潜力。

Method: 构建了一个基于FLORES+数据集的对比数据集，包含1,850个英-希翻译对，由两名翻译专家审核，涵盖10类核心与5类探索性错误。评估五种多语言嵌入模型与一个参考无的COMETKiwi模型，通过余弦相似度计算源句与其正确/错误译文间的相似性，以对比准确率和得分差值衡量各类错误的敏感性。

Result: BGE-M3在嵌入模型中表现最优（准确率89.30%），COMETKiwi整体最高（94.49%）。嵌入模型对事实性、词汇语义错误检测更可靠，对时态、代词指代、语义角色等错误敏感性较低；COMETKiwi在时态、指代、语义角色错误上优于嵌入模型，但在日期时间与数字错误上表现不佳。各模型表现出互补性。

Conclusion: 多语言句子嵌入可提供有用的语义充分性信号，但更适合集成到更全面的翻译评估框架中，而非作为独立的评价指标使用。

Abstract: Multilingual sentence embeddings are increasingly used to estimate semantic similarity across languages, yet their sensitivity to fine-grained translation errors remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether general-purpose multilingual embedding models can distinguish correct English-Greek translations from minimally modified erroneous alternatives. A contrastive dataset was developed from FLORES+ sentence-aligned reference translations and reviewed by two translation experts. It contains 1,850 examples across ten core and five exploratory error categories, covering factual, lexical-semantic, grammatical, relational, referential, and discourse-level phenomena.
  Five multilingual sentence-embedding models (BGE-M3, Multilingual E5, Multilingual MPNet, LaBSE, and Jina Embeddings v3) were evaluated using cosine similarity between each English source sentence and its correct and erroneous Greek translations. A reference-free COMETKiwi model was also evaluated as an MT quality-estimation baseline. Performance was assessed through contrastive accuracy and score margins for category-specific sensitivity. BGE-M3 achieved the highest accuracy among embedding models at 89.30 percent, while COMETKiwi achieved 94.49 percent. Embedding models detected explicit factual and lexical changes more reliably than tense-and-aspect and pronoun-coreference errors. COMETKiwi improved performance on several difficult categories, including tense and aspect, pronoun and coreference, and semantic-role errors, but showed lower sensitivity to date-and-time errors and underperformed the embedding models on numbers. The results show complementary error-sensitivity profiles: multilingual sentence embeddings provide useful semantic adequacy signals but are better suited as components of broader translation-evaluation frameworks than as standalone metrics.

</details>


### [66] [A rigor-matched audit of periodic-step layer skipping for efficient llm inference: conflayers versus swift, with a supplemental analysis of trained routing alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28846)
*Prateek Kumar Sikdar,Arpan Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对两种基于搜索的分层跳过方法（ConfLayers和SWIFT）及标准自回归解码在不同模型规模（Qwen2.5-0.5B和Qwen2.5-1.5B）和任务（GSM8K推理与CNN/DailyMail摘要）下进行了严格对比。结果表明，SWIFT在四项测试中三项准确率领先，且在去除在线搜索开销后，其实际推理速度优于ConfLayers（快5%-21%），而后者在GSM8K任务上表现显著落后。尽管SWIFT的搜索开销更大且波动性更高（最高达28.7%），但整体效率仍占优。此外，训练路由方法（LayerRoute和LayerDrop）虽带来小幅加速（1.08-1.33倍），但准确率严重下降，尤其在复杂任务上近乎崩溃。研究提供了一套可复用的审计协议模板，以推动高效推理方法的严谨评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层跳过方法在效率与准确性之间存在权衡，尤其在复杂任务中表现不稳定；亟需一套统一、严格的评估框架来公平比较不同方法的真实性能，避免因评估偏差导致误导性结论。

Method: 采用三种子验证的严格审计流程，对ConfLayers、SWIFT、LayerRoute、LayerDrop及标准自回归解码进行跨模型、跨任务的对比实验。通过分离在线搜索开销与纯推理成本，量化真实推理效率，并在真实输入门控条件下评估各方法的准确性和速度。

Result: SWIFT在准确率和实际推理速度上均优于ConfLayers，尤其在复杂推理任务中优势明显；训练路由方法虽有加速但准确率大幅下降，尤其在高难度任务上表现极差。所有方法在真实评估条件下的性能差异显著，凸显了严格评估的重要性。

Conclusion: SWIFT是当前最有效的在线层跳过方法，其在准确率和效率上的综合表现优于其他方法。研究强调应采用严谨、一致的评估协议，以确保对大语言模型推理优化技术的真实性能评估，避免误导性结论。

Abstract: Layer-skipping methods for efficient LLM inference decide, at some granularity, which transformer layers to execute for a given input. We present a rigor-matched, three-seed audit of two periodic-step, search-based methods that make this decision online at inference time and re-evaluate it every few generation steps: a confidence-gated early-exit baseline (ConfLayers) and genuine self-speculative decoding (SWIFT, Xia et al. 2024), together with vanilla autoregressive decoding, across two model scales (Qwen2.5-0.5B and Qwen2.5-1.5B) and two tasks (GSM8K reasoning and CNN/DailyMail summarization). SWIFT is the strongest method on accuracy in three of four cells; ConfLayers is dominated everywhere, with particularly large deficits on GSM8K at 1.5B. Once online-search overhead is separated from pure inference cost, SWIFT's true inference speed is faster than ConfLayers's in all four cells (5-21%), reversing the naive wall-clock ranking in three of them. ConfLayers's search overhead is small and stable (1-2% of cost), while SWIFT's is larger and more variable (up to 28.7%). We additionally examine two trained-routing methods, LayerRoute (Sikdar, 2026) and LayerDrop (Fan et al. 2020), as a supplemental analysis because they operate at coarser decision granularities. Under a verified protocol with genuine per-input gating, a genuine full-model baseline, and genuine inference-time compute skipping, both show modest speedups (1.08-1.33x) but accuracy well below the periodic-step methods, including a near-total collapse for LayerRoute on GSM8K at 1.5B (0.003 mean exact-match across three seeds). We release the full audit protocol as a template for rigor-matched efficiency comparisons.

</details>


### [67] [Latent-Space Intervention for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency: Consistency Improvements without Accuracy Drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28860)
*Faeze Ghorbanpour,Constanza Fierro,Alexander Fraser,Anders Sogaard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了通过跨语言潜在空间干预来减少大语言模型在不同语言间回答同一事实问题时的不一致性。通过在并行多语言表示上训练分层自编码器，并在推理时对事实问答提示进行修正，发现潜在空间干预能显著提升语言间的几何对齐性，并在开放问答和多项选择问答格式中均实现跨语言一致性提升，且不损害事实准确性。开放问答中，英语与非英语语言之间的斯皮尔曼等级相关性显著提高（如英-阿拉伯语对提升0.16，英-俄语对提升0.20）；多项选择问答中，答案与英语的一致性在KLAR和mParaRel数据集上均稳定提升。消融实验表明，自编码器重建带来一致收益且无准确率损失，主成分投影贡献较小，均值偏移在开放问答中提升显著但以牺牲部分准确率为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不同语言间对同一事实问题的回答存在不一致性，影响跨语言任务的可靠性，亟需一种有效方法提升跨语言回答的一致性。

Method: 在并行多语言表示上训练分层自编码器，并在推理阶段对事实问答提示进行潜在空间干预修正，通过几何对齐优化实现跨语言一致性提升。

Result: 潜在空间干预显著提升了语言间的几何对齐性，在开放问答和多项选择问答中均实现跨语言一致性提升，且未降低事实准确性；开放问答中斯皮尔曼相关性提升显著（英-阿拉伯语+0.16，英-俄语+0.20），多项选择问答中答案一致性在多个基准上持续改善。

Conclusion: 跨语言潜在空间干预是一种有效提升大语言模型跨语言一致性的方式，可在不损害准确性的情况下显著改善多语言问答的一致性表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often answer the same factual question differently across languages. We study whether cross-lingual latent-space intervention can reduce this inconsistency. We train layer-specific autoencoders on parallel multilingual representations and apply inference-time corrections to factual QA prompts. We find that latent intervention improves geometric alignment between languages, and that this improvement translates into consistent gains in cross-lingual consistency with English across both open-ended and multiple-choice QA formats, without degrading factual accuracy. In open-ended QA, Spearman's rank correlation between English and non-English languages improves substantially, with gains of 0.16 for English-Arabic and 0.20 for English-Russian pairs. In multiple-choice QA, answer agreement with English improves consistently across both KLAR and mParaRel. Ablations show that AE reconstruction yields consistent gains at no accuracy cost, while PCA projection contributes marginally, and mean-shift produces substantially larger consistency gains in open-ended QA at the cost of some accuracy.

</details>


### [68] [No Detectable Change in Side-Level WER from Prompt-Level Context: A Preregistered Ablation on a Production Oral-History Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28875)
*Theodore O. Cochran,Stephanie Dodson,Keith Nore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在生产级口述历史转录工具中测试了推理时提供上下文对大模型的影响，发现完整上下文并未显著降低词错误率（WER），且四个预注册假设均未得到支持。实验采用配对消融设计，使用19段1970-80年代的退化录音进行三类提示处理，交叉两种商用模型（gpt-4o-transcribe 和 gemini-2.5-flash）。结果表明，gpt-4o-transcribe 的中位配对差异为+0.6 WER点，置信区间为[-1.1, +1.0]；Gemini 结果不稳，无法得出有效结论。事后分析显示，运行间流水线变异性高于确认差异，因此单次转录无法分辨此类效应。审计验证了上下文机制实际生效，序列对齐分析发现仅在完整列出短语上略有改善，但不足以改变整体WER，且Gemini还伴随未列出词项错误增加。研究建议需结合逐项、插入和说话人标签等细粒度指标评估上下文机制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在推理阶段通过提示上下文来低成本适应大模型语音转录至特定领域，尤其是已有小模型研究显示显著提升的情况下，验证该方法在真实生产环境中的有效性。

Method: 采用配对消融实验设计，对19段来自生产语料库的口述历史录音，在三种提示条件（全上下文、无上下文、部分上下文）下，分别使用gpt-4o-transcribe与gemini-2.5-flash两种模型进行重处理，并基于人工校正的逐字参考文本进行评分。所有分析代码在确认批次评分前已哈希冻结，确保可重复性。同时进行了实现审计与序列对齐分析以验证上下文影响的真实性与具体表现。

Result: gpt-4o-transcribe 在全上下文与无上下文之间的中位词错误率差异为+0.6，置信区间为[-1.1, +1.0]，无统计显著性；Gemini 由于结果不稳定，无法支持有效推断。事后分析表明运行间变异性超过实验差异，单次转录无法区分微小变化。序列对齐分析显示仅在完全列出的短语上有轻微改进，但不足以影响整体侧级WER，且Gemini出现未列出词项错误上升现象。

Conclusion: 在当前生产系统中，推理时添加完整提示上下文并未带来可检测的词错误率改善。评估上下文机制的有效性必须依赖更细粒度的指标，如逐项、插入和说话人标签误差，而不能仅依赖聚合准确率。此外，运行间变异性可能掩盖微小但有意义的改进，需在设计中加以控制。

Abstract: Supplying context at inference time to a large multimodal model is an inexpensive lever for adapting speech transcription to a domain, and earlier results on smaller models reported large gains. This work tested that mechanism where it ships, in the prompt-conditioning layer of a production oral-history transcription tool, on a sample from its own production corpus. Full prompt-level context did not detectably change side-level word error rate (WER), and none of the four preregistered hypotheses was supported.
