The design was a within-item paired ablation, preregistered with the analysis code frozen by hash before the confirmatory batch was scored; two disclosed gpt-4o pilot sides had been scored earlier, during scorer development. Nineteen cassette sides, about 10.6 hours of degraded 1970s-80s interview audio, were reprocessed through the production code path under three prompt arms, crossed with two deployed commercial configurations, gpt-4o-transcribe and gemini-2.5-flash, and scored against operator-corrected verbatim references. For gpt-4o-transcribe the median paired difference between the full-context and no-context arms was +0.6 WER points, with a side-resampled interval of [-1.1, +1.0]; the Gemini estimates were too unstable to support a comparable negative inference. A post-hoc rerun found run-to-run pipeline variability larger than the confirmatory differences, so effects of that size cannot be resolved from one transcription per cell. An implementation audit verified the manipulation was live, and sequence-alignment analysis found a small improvement on complete context-listed phrases, too small to materially change side-level WER, and for Gemini coexisting with worsened unlisted-token error. Evaluating context mechanisms therefore requires sequence-aligned term-level, insertion, and speaker-label measures alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [69] [Moving the Mean Toward the Known Good, Not Beyond It: What Inference-Time Interventions and Weight Consolidation Buy in Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28886)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过生成-验证-选择-LoRA整合的循环，在在线装箱问题上探索模型自我验证成功经验的学习效果。结果显示，基于价值筛选的候选样本训练使模型在未见变体上的表现更接近价值目标（减少1.7分过剩，p=0.008），且最佳候选结果收敛于经典启发式算法水平，不再提升。三次独立重复实验均显示结果高度一致（均值-2.0, -1.8, -1.9），所有可评估变体中7个均支持整合策略（p=0.008）。尽管整合降低了优于经典算法的候选比例（10%降至3.9%），但因产出量大，绝对数量仍更多（5对1）。对比实验表明，监督微调控制组显示集中效应源于监督锚定而非对劣质候选的排斥（96%候选精确达到经典水平）。结论：模型可通过自我验证机制稳定优化表现，但难以超越经典方法；高质量候选的平均质量可复制，最佳表现亦被锁定在经典水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型在生成-验证-选择-整合循环中，通过自身验证的成功经验进行学习时，能否突破经典启发式方法的性能上限。核心动机来自推理阶段的‘审计日志’观察：模型自动生成的概要能有效促进文档整合，而内嵌验证器的流式设计被成功模仿，每笔记簿产生16.4条虚构判决。这提示我们，模型可通过结构化反馈机制实现质量提升，但其极限是否可超越经典方法仍待检验。

Method: 采用生成-验证-选择-LoRA整合的迭代循环，在在线装箱问题上训练模型。通过价值过滤筛选候选解，进行LoRA参数微调以固化成功模式。设置三组独立实验（含随机种子与未见测试集），并引入监督微调对照组（SFT-only）与随机整合对照组，评估不同策略下模型在未见变体上的表现差异。通过多次重复和聚合分析验证结果稳健性。

Result: 价值筛选的整合策略显著提升模型在未见变体上的表现，平均超额成本降低1.7点（p=0.008），且优于随机整合（-3.1，p=0.004）。三次独立实验结果高度一致（均值-2.0, -1.8, -1.9），所有可评估变体中7个均支持整合策略（p=0.008）。最佳候选解精确趋近经典启发式水平（0.021028），且在所有实验中均未超越。虽然整合降低了优于经典算法的候选比例（10%→3.9%），但因产量增加，绝对数量仍更高（5对1）。监督微调对照组显示96%候选精确落在经典水平，说明集中效应源于监督锚定而非排斥劣解。

Conclusion: 模型通过自我验证的生成-整合循环可稳定提升性能，使最优解逼近经典启发式水平，但尚未发现其能持续超越经典方法的能力。高质量候选的平均质量可被复制，但最佳表现被锁定在经典水平。该机制虽能放大有效模式，但受限于初始引导和反馈结构，难以突破既定边界。

Abstract: What does a generation loop gain from learning on its own verified successes? In cycles of generate, verify, select and LoRA-consolidate on online bin packing, training on value-filtered candidates shifts what the model writes on held-out variants toward value (-1.7 points of excess, p=0.008; -3.1 against a random-consolidation control, p=0.004) while the best observed candidate converges to the classic heuristic's level and no further. A confirmation battery replicates the whole procedure three times, with fresh seeds and a never-consulted held-out set read exactly once: the mean was nearly identical in all three lineages (-2.0, -1.8, -1.9), and after aggregating within held-out variant all seven evaluable variants favored consolidation (p=0.008). The best observed candidate moved to the classic heuristic's level, exactly (0.021028 in all three lineages, for attract and for the random control alike), and never beyond it. A matched SFT-only control shows the supervised anchor, not repulsion from bad candidates, does the concentrating (96% of candidates land exactly at the classic heuristic's level). The tails cut both ways: consolidation lowers the per-candidate rate of better-than-classic candidates (10% to 3.9%) while its larger production yields more such candidates absolutely (5 against 1, on few events). As motivation we report the inference-time ledger that led here: a model-written schematic recap buys judged document integration and nothing buys development; a verifier written into the stream is imitated, 16.4 fabricated verdict lines per notebook. Mean quality among valid candidates can be bought and replicated; the observed best goes to the classic and, so far, never beyond it.

</details>


### [70] [VoiceCodeBench: Evaluating Exact Structured-Token Recovery in Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28916)
*Tyler Baumgartner,Brandon Tai,Lisa Kaelin-Martin,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VoiceCodeBench基准，用于评估英语ASR系统在精确恢复结构化标记方面的性能。该基准包含300段人工录制的工作场景音频，覆盖8个领域，共1,482个需精确恢复的目标实体，涵盖26种类型。在仅提供原始音频的条件下，评估指标包括CTEM、TSR和各类型精确恢复率。结果显示，虽然低WER通常对应较好结构化恢复，但相关性仅为-0.73，且最强基线系统任务成功率仅达68.7%，表明现有ASR系统在关键数值或标识符恢复上仍有显著不足，亟需引入更精细的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估依赖词错误率（WER），但许多语音工作流依赖于精确的标识符、路径和测量值等结构化信息。即使转录文本流畅且WER较低，仍可能因数值或标识符错误导致下游系统故障，因此需要更精确的评估方法来衡量结构化内容的准确恢复能力。

Method: 构建VoiceCodeBench基准，包含300段真实工作场景录音，覆盖8个领域，共1,482个需精确还原的实体，每类实体有标准写法。采用仅输入原始音频的协议，不提供额外上下文或元数据。评估指标包括：Canonical Token/Entity Match (CTEM)、Task Success Rate (TSR)以及按类型划分的精确恢复率。

Result: 12个基线ASR系统中，WER与CTEM、TSR的Spearman相关系数分别为-0.73，表明低WER虽有助于结构化恢复，但不能完全决定其表现。最强系统任务成功率仅为68.7%，近三分之一的录音存在至少一个关键值未被正确恢复，说明当前系统在精确恢复结构化信息方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: 现有基于WER的评估不足以反映ASR系统在生产环境中对关键结构化信息的处理能力。必须引入如CTEM、TSR等实体敏感型指标，才能有效评估系统是否能准确恢复必须被解析、存储、比较或执行的精确值，从而保障下游系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems are commonly evaluated with word error rate (WER), yet many voice workflows depend on exact written values for identifiers, paths, and measured quantities. A transcript can appear fluent and achieve low WER while corrupting a value that a downstream system must parse, store, or execute.
  We introduce VoiceCodeBench, a benchmark for evaluating exact structured-token recovery in English ASR. It contains 300 human-recorded workplace segments spanning eight workflow domains and 1,482 audited target entities across 26 entity types, each with a canonical written form recoverable from the audio. Under a raw-audio-only protocol, systems receive audio bytes without additional context or metadata. Alongside WER, we evaluate Canonical Token/Entity Match (CTEM), Task Success Rate (TSR), and per-type exact recovery.
  Across 12 baseline ASR systems, lower WER generally corresponded to better structured-token recovery but did not fully determine it: Spearman correlations were -0.73 for both WER versus CTEM and WER versus TSR. The strongest baseline by TSR reached only 68.7%, leaving nearly one third of recordings with at least one unrecovered workflow-critical value. These results show that entity-sensitive metrics are needed to assess whether ASR output preserves exact values that production systems must parse, route, store, compare, or execute.

</details>


### [71] [Causal Interventions Reveal Typologically Organized Syntactic Mechanisms in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28924)
*Sasha Boguraev,Toshiki Nakai,Kyle Mahowald,Julius Steuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用机制可解释性技术研究多语言语言模型中的跨语言句法机制转移问题。通过分离语言内部机制并尝试跨语言迁移，发现在四种模型和三种句法结构（主谓数一致、回指代词性别一致、填空-缺口宾语提取）中存在一致的跨语言机制转移现象，且迁移程度与语言类型相似性正相关。研究为跨语言句法结构和多语言处理提供了新假设，并展示了语言模型研究对语言学理论的启发作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，语言学理论认识到跨语言句法规律的存在，但缺乏精细可控的人类处理机制实验手段，难以实证检验这些规律是否由相似机制处理。本研究旨在借助语言模型的可解释性工具，探索跨语言句法机制是否共享并可迁移。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，先在单个语言中识别内部句法处理机制，再尝试将这些机制迁移到其他语言中进行验证。研究涵盖四种多语言语言模型和三种典型句法现象。

Result: 在所有测试的语言和模型中均观察到跨语言机制转移现象；迁移程度呈梯度分布，且与语言类型相似性显著相关。

Conclusion: 本研究支持跨语言句法结构可能由相似机制处理的观点，为语言学理论提供了新的实证依据，并展示了语言模型在揭示人类语言处理机制方面的潜力。

Abstract: Linguistic theory has long recognized cross-linguistic syntactic regularities, leading to claims that these similar structures are processed by similar mechanisms. However, this hypothesis has been difficult to test empirically due to our lack of fine-grained, manipulable access of human processing mechanisms. In this work, we take advantage of techniques from mechanistic interpretability to study such a question in multilingual LMs. We first isolate language-internal mechanisms before attempting to transfer them cross-lingually. Across four models and three well-studied constructions (subject--verb number agreement, anaphoric pronoun gender agreement, and filler--gap object extraction) we find consistent cross-lingual mechanism transfer. We further find transfer to be graded, with more transfer between more typologically similar languages. We believe our work provides novel hypotheses about cross-linguistic syntactic structures and multilingual processing, and more broadly shows how the study of language models can help inform linguistic theory.

</details>


### [72] [Leveraging Turn-taking Dynamics for Intent Recognition in Multi-party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28926)
*Galo Castillo-López,Alexis Lombard,Gaël de Chalendar,Nasredine Semmar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务学习方法，用于多方对话意图识别，引入一个辅助任务来建模对话中的轮流互动动态。通过计算说话人转换序列的转移动态熵（turn-transition entropy）作为自监督目标，量化交互模式的可预测性。实验表明，引入该辅助任务能显著提升意图识别性能，优于忽略多方互动动态的现有方法。结果还显示，该连续目标可作为单任务目标进行学习，证明其携带了有效信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多方对话意图识别中忽视了对话中的轮流互动动态，而这些动态对理解对话意图至关重要。因此，需要一种能够捕捉和利用交互模式变化的方法，以提升意图识别的准确性。

Method: 提出基于转移动态熵的自监督辅助任务，利用说话人转换序列计算熵值作为连续目标，与主任务（意图识别）联合训练，采用多任务学习框架。

Result: 在多个预训练模型上的实验结果显示，加入该辅助任务后，意图识别性能显著提升，且该连续目标可作为单一任务目标有效学习，表明其蕴含有用信息。

Conclusion: 所提出的多任务学习框架通过引入转移动态熵作为辅助任务，有效捕捉多方对话中的交互动态，显著提升了意图识别性能，验证了其在建模复杂对话结构方面的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a multi-task learning approach for multi-party dialogue intent recognition that leverages an auxiliary task that models turn-taking dynamics. Specifically, we introduce turn-transition entropy, a self-supervised target computed from the sequence of speaker transitions, which quantifies the predictability of interaction patterns. Experiments on multiple pre-trained models demonstrate that incorporating this auxiliary task improves intent recognition performance, outperforming existing approaches which ignore multi-party interaction dynamics. We find that our proposed continuous target can be learned as a single-task objective, suggesting that it is an actual signal carrying useful information.

</details>


### [73] [The Hallucination Signal Is a Mean Shift: Why Simple Probes Suffice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28930)
*Jungseob Lee,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，大型语言模型（LLM）幻觉检测中的信号主要由单一的均值偏移分量主导，去除该方向后检测性能降至随机水平。尽管现有探测器架构日益复杂，但其效果差异主要源于高维协方差估计困难，而非非线性特征的可利用性。简单的L2正则化逻辑回归（AUROC=0.952）在性能上优于或等同于12种受控的复杂架构，并且多层聚合方法在匹配设定下超越了CLAP跨层注意力探测。由于信号分布于连续的层带，LayerMix通过聚合实现与已知最优层相当的性能，而无需访问最优层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前隐藏状态探测器虽能有效检测大模型幻觉，但其信号几何结构尚未被充分理解，导致探测架构不断复杂化。本文旨在揭示探测信号的本质几何特性，以解释为何复杂架构看似必要，从而指导更高效、简洁的探测设计。

Method: 在三个7B规模模型和三个数据集上，采用配对示例范式进行实验。通过分析隐藏状态空间中的判别信号，使用收缩线性判别分析（Shrinkage LDA）评估维度贡献，比较不同探测架构的性能，包括简单线性模型与复杂非线性架构。引入LayerMix方法，通过多层聚合捕捉连续层带中的信号，实现接近理想层的探测效果。

Result: 信号几乎完全由单一均值偏移成分主导；移除该方向后检测性能降至随机水平。收缩线性判别分析可解决约73%的维度损失问题。简单L2正则化逻辑回归（AUROC=0.952）表现优异，优于或等同于12种复杂架构。多层聚合的LayerMix方法在无须访问最优层的情况下达到或超过基准性能。

Conclusion: LLM幻觉检测中的信号具有高度简化的几何结构，主要体现为单一直线性均值偏移。复杂探测架构的优越性很大程度上源于高维协方差估计的挑战，而非非线性结构的利用。因此，简单线性模型配合有效的多层聚合即可实现高性能探测，为未来设计提供更高效、可解释的方向。

Abstract: Hidden-state probes effectively detect LLM hallucinations, but the geometry of the signal remains poorly characterized, driving increasingly complex probe architectures. Across three 7B-scale models and three datasets in a paired-example paradigm, we find the signal overwhelmingly dominated by a single mean-shift component, and removing this direction collapses detection to chance. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis closes about 73% of the gap between 1D and full-dimensional classifiers, so apparent architectural complexity largely reflects high-dimensional covariance estimation difficulty rather than exploitable non-linearity. A simple L2-regularized logistic regression (0.952 AUROC) bounds or outperforms twelve controlled architectural alternatives, and our multi-layer aggregation exceeds CLAP cross-layer attention probing under matched paradigm. Because the signal spans a contiguous layer band, LayerMix aggregates it to match oracle-layer performance without oracle access. Our claims characterize the geometry within the controlled paired-example paradigm. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/LayerMix.

</details>


### [74] [VocalAffectBench: Evaluating Vocal Emotion Recognition in AI Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28932)
*Models Luc Debaupte,Tyler Baumgartner,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VocalAffectBench 是一个公开的、仅测试基准，用于评估AI音频模型从原始音频中识别表达性语音情绪的能力。该基准包含273段英语语音片段，覆盖七种情绪类别（愤怒、厌恶、恐惧、快乐、中性、悲伤、惊讶），所有基线模型均仅基于音频进行评估。六种基线平均准确率为35.5%，最强模型gemini_3_5_flash达到46.5%，仍远低于稳健水平。在更粗粒度的情感极性分类（正、中性、负）中，平均准确率为50.9%。模型对中性情绪识别最准确，而对惊讶和恐惧情绪识别较差，表明当前模型在捕捉非中性情感方面仍十分脆弱，尤其在语音助手等实际应用场景中表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音产品需要理解说话人的情绪线索，但传统文本转录无法提供这些信息。因此，亟需一个专门评估语音情绪识别能力的基准，以推动相关技术发展。

Method: 构建VocalAffectBench，包含273段人类录制的英语音频片段，共1.95小时，涵盖七类情绪标签。所有模型仅使用原始音频进行评估，不依赖文本或上下文信息。通过多模型对比分析，评估其在细粒度与粗粒度情绪分类任务中的表现。

Result: 六种基线模型平均准确率为35.5%，最强模型为46.5%；在情感极性分类中，平均准确率为50.9%。中性情绪识别召回率最高（75.6%），而惊讶和恐惧最低（分别为10.7%和15.4%），显示模型对非中性情绪识别能力薄弱。

Conclusion: 尽管当前AI音频模型能从语音中提取部分情绪信号，但离实现稳健的离散情绪识别仍有显著差距，尤其在关键的非中性情绪上表现不佳，亟需进一步研究与改进。

Abstract: Voice products increasingly need affective cues that are present in speech but absent from transcripts. We introduce VocalAffectBench, a public, test-only benchmark for evaluating whether AI audio models can identify expressed vocal emotion from raw audio. The benchmark contains 273 human-recorded English WAV clips from 51 speaker accounts totaling 1.95 hours across seven labels: angry, disgusted, fearful, happy, neutral, sad, and surprised, with 39 clips per class. All baselines are evaluated from audio alone, without transcripts or contextual metadata. Across six released baselines, average accuracy is 35.5%. The strongest baseline, gemini_3_5_flash, reaches 46.5% on the seven-way task, above the 14.3% random baseline but far from robust emotion recognition. A secondary valence-bucket analysis maps labels into positive, neutral, and negative classes, excluding surprised because its valence is ambiguous. Aggregate accuracy under this coarser view is 50.9%. Performance is highly uneven across classes. By recall, neutral is identified most reliably at 75.6% averaged across baselines, while surprised and fearful reach only 10.7% and 15.4%, respectively. These results show that the evaluated baselines can extract some affective signal from speech, but discrete expressed-emotion recognition remains fragile, especially for non-neutral emotions that are often most important in voice agent workflows.

</details>


### [75] [CoVA-SFT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Chain of Visual Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28958)
*Tsung-Han Wu,Heekyung Lee,Anya Ji,Haoming Chen,Trevor Darrell,Joseph E. Gonzalez,David M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoVA-SFT和CoVA-Bench，构建一个包含51.9K样本、超过222K个多模态推理步骤的大规模结构化语料库，用于训练模型在文本推理中构建和维护内部视觉工作空间。通过显式推理表述、代理渲染和验证循环，提升模型在多模态推理中的表现，实验表明微调后的模型在基准测试上性能优于现有混合推理基线，但仍不及纯文本CoT基线，揭示未来研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本仅有的链式思维（CoT）方法在处理视觉问题时存在将视觉信息强行转化为冗长文字的缺陷；尽管有架构支持多模态输入，但缺乏大规模、多步骤、自校正的训练数据来指导模型构建和维护内部视觉工作空间。

Method: 构建CoVA-SFT语料库，包含51.9K样本、222K个推理步骤，覆盖5种布局类型和17个复杂任务；设计CoVA-Bench基准测试集，包含1,700个独立测试样本，用于可复现评估；引入显式推理表述、代理渲染与验证机制，使模型能够融合文本与视觉抽象进行推理。

Result: 在CoVA-Bench上，经过CoVA-SFT微调的模型平均性能优于所有混合式链式思维基线两倍以上，但仍未达到强文本仅有的链式思维基线水平，说明当前方法仍有局限性。

Conclusion: CoVA-SFT与CoVA-Bench为多模态链式思维提供了重要资源，推动了模型在文本推理中利用视觉抽象的能力，但如何有效整合视觉与语言推理仍面临挑战，需进一步探索。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has dramatically improved large language models (LLMs) by allowing them to decompose problems into intermediate steps. While CoT is widely effective for linguistic tasks, text-only CoT forces models to serialize visual problems into awkward prose. Although architectural solutions exist to process visual inputs, the community lacks a massive, multi-step, self-corrected dataset to teach models how to build and maintain internal visual workspaces when solving purely textual reasoning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce CoVA-SFT, a highly structured corpus of 51.9K samples containing over 222K multimodal reasoning steps across 5 distinct layout families and 17 complex tasks, and CoVA-Bench, a companion benchmark of 1,700 held-out test samples spanning the same tasks for reproducible evaluation. By providing explicit rationale formulations, agentic renderings, and verification loops, CoVA-SFT teaches multimodal language models to interleave text and visual abstractions. We validate the dataset by demonstrating that models fine-tuned on CoVA-SFT outperform all interleaved CoT baselines by more than 2x on average on CoVA-Bench, though they still fall short of strong text-only CoT baselines, highlighting open challenges for future work.

</details>


### [76] [The Illusion of Replacement: Rethinking Specialized Machine Learning Models in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28980)
*Kiyan Rezaee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析2016-2026年间涵盖九种模态的159篇论文，探讨了语言模型是否能取代传统针对结构化数据设计的专用架构。研究发现，在极少数样本预测、符号任务、文本标注的知识图谱及大规模单模态预训练等特定场景下，语言模型表现具有竞争力；但当结构表示与计算被直接评估时，未见语言模型普遍替代专用架构的证据。相反，多个独立研究社区均表现出相同模式：当语言模型能力不足时，会通过图模块、结构化标记、专用注意力机制等非语言组件重新引入结构。因此，专业化并未消失，而是转移了形式。尽管语言模型随规模扩大性能提升，其与结构感知架构之间的差距能否最终消除仍未经验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型能否全面替代传统为结构化数据设计的专用架构，尤其在结构表示与计算方面的能力是否足以支撑通用性替代。

Method: 对2016–2026年间的159篇相关论文进行系统综述，覆盖九种模态，基于预测准确性、结构表示与计算效率进行分类分析，将现有方法归纳为八类表征范式，从纯语言模型到完全专用架构。

Result: 语言模型在特定任务中表现优异，但在需要显式结构表示或计算的任务中，仍需依赖图模块、结构化标记等非语言组件补充结构信息；结构化成分并未消失，而是以新形式重构。

Conclusion: 语言模型虽在部分场景下表现强劲，但无法普遍替代专用架构。结构化能力的缺失通常通过引入非语言组件来弥补，说明专业化更多是形式迁移而非消亡。模型规模扩展虽提升性能，但是否能完全弥合与结构感知架构的差距尚无实证。

Abstract: Can the specialized architectures that machine learning has traditionally built for structured data be replaced by language-based models? This question is examined through a review of 159 papers (2016--2026) across nine modalities, with predictive accuracy considered alongside structural representation and computation. A distinction is made between performing a task and preserving and computing the structure that makes the task tractable, and existing approaches are organized into eight representational regimes, ranging from language-only systems to fully specialized architectures. Language-mediated models are found to be highly competitive in specific settings, including extreme few-shot prediction, discretized symbolic tasks, textually annotated knowledge graphs, and large-scale single-modality pretraining. However, whenever structural representation or computation is directly evaluated rather than accuracy alone, no evidence of general architectural replacement is found. Instead, a recurring pattern is observed across independent research communities: when language alone is insufficient, the missing structure is reintroduced through a graph module, structural tokens, specialized attention, or another non-linguistic component. In this sense, specialization more often relocates than disappears. Moreover, although performance of language-based models is improved by scaling, whether the gap to a structure-aware architecture can eventually be eliminated remains untested.

</details>


### [77] [Detecting and Guiding LLM-Generated Korean Poetry with Interpretable Form-level Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28986)
*Keunhyeung Park,Seunguk Yu,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对大语言模型（LLM）在现代韩语诗歌生成中表现不佳的问题，提出了一种基于四个形式层面语言特征的分析框架，包括输出长度（体积）、行尾形式的多样性与连贯性（结构变化）、行长不规则性（节奏不规则性）以及标准拼写的遵守度（规范一致性）。通过五个可解释的特征，实现了对韩语诗是否由人类或LLM创作的检测，并在零样本跨模型检测中达到83.60的平均AUC-ROC，优于最强基线方法KatFishNet的75.84，提升显著。同时，引导生成时，专家评估和模型分析均显示，基于这些特征指导生成的诗歌更接近人类风格，尤其在长度、节奏和结尾模式上表现出向人类分布靠拢的趋势。研究证明，可解释的语言特定特征可在诊断与引导LLM诗歌生成方面发挥有效作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成现代韩语诗歌时常产生类似‘分行散文’的输出，缺乏诗歌应有的形式美感。现有方法难以有效区分人类与机器创作的诗歌，也无法精准引导模型生成更符合人类诗歌风格的内容。因此亟需一种可解释、语言特定的评估与生成框架，以弥合人机诗歌之间的形式差距。

Method: 构建并量化四个形式层面的语言维度：输出长度、结构变化、节奏不规则性和规范一致性，将其转化为五个可解释的特征。采用逻辑回归分类器进行零样本跨模型的作者识别，同时在生成阶段引入这些特征作为约束条件，通过专家评估与统计分析验证生成效果。

Result: 在七种未见大语言模型上的零样本检测中，分类器达到83.60的平均AUC-ROC，比基线方法提升7.76点，相对改进10.23%；专家评价表明，特征引导生成的诗歌更受青睐；多模型分析显示，生成内容在长度、节奏和结尾分布上更接近人类诗歌。

Conclusion: 可解释、语言特定的特征能够有效实现对大语言模型生成韩语诗歌的形式诊断与引导，为提升生成质量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with modern Korean poetry, producing outputs that resemble "line-broken prose." We address two coupled tasks: detecting whether a Korean poem is human- or LLM-authored, and guiding LLMs to generate poetry closer in form to human writing. We quantify the human-LLM gap along four form-level linguistic dimensions: output length (Volume), the diversity and connective use of line-final forms (Structure Variation), the irregularity of line lengths (Rhythmic Irregularity), and adherence to standard orthography (Normative Adherence). We operationalize these dimensions as five interpretable features. For detection, a logistic regression classifier over these five features attains an average AUC-ROC of 83.60 in zero-shot out-of-distribution detection across seven unseen LLMs, versus 75.84 for the strongest baseline in our comparison, KatFishNet, an absolute gain of 7.76 AUC points and a 10.23% relative improvement; one generator-specific punctuation pattern outside our taxonomy remains a boundary case. For generation, expert evaluation on GPT-5.2 prefers feature-guided poems over the unconstrained baseline, and analyses across GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3 show that targeted length, rhythm, and ending statistics move toward the human distribution. These results suggest that interpretable, language-specific features can bridge the diagnosis and guidance of LLM-generated poetry.

</details>


### [78] [A Unifying Perspective on Language Model Representations: From Filler-Role Structure to Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29034)
*Zhang Enyan,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用张量积表示（TPRs）作为统一假设，以解释语言模型的可解释性方法。TPRs通过填充-角色绑定的方式在向量空间中表示组合结构，并能数学和实证上统一多种现有可解释性方法，如加性类比、线性探测、稀疏自编码器和激活修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型可解释性方法各自孤立，缺乏对底层结构的统一理解，因此需要一个能够整合多种方法的理论框架。

Method: 提出并验证张量积表示（TPRs）作为统一框架，从数学上推导出多种可解释性方法的共同来源，并在不同规模的模型上进行实证测试。

Result: TPRs能够有效统一多种可解释性方法；构建的衍生版本在性能上与标准版本相当，证明了该框架的可行性与一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究为可解释性提供了一个统一视角，不仅解释了单个观察结果，还揭示了不同方法之间的内在联系，是迈向全面理解神经网络本质的重要一步。

Abstract: A wide range of methods have been proposed for interpreting language models, delivering important insights into their inner workings. However, different methods and their resulting insights stand in relative isolation: what could the underlying structure of language models be, such that they give rise to all our interpretations? In this work, we propose using Tensor Product Representations (TPRs) as a unifying hypothesis. TPRs give a concrete proposal for how compositional structure could be represented in vector space --- as filler-role bindings. We show, both mathematically and empirically, that TPRs can unify several prior interpretability methods: additive analogies, linear probing, sparse autoencoders, and activation patching. Mathematically, we show that these methods can all be derived from TPRs. Empirically, we apply the derivations to a range of different models --- from small toy models to LLMs --- to construct instances of each of the above interpretability methods; these constructed variants perform comparably to their standard variants. We view this work as a step toward what interpretability will ideally provide: a unified account of the nature of neural networks, corroborated not just by individual observations but also by an explanation of the connections between them.

</details>


### [79] [RouteSparse: Input-Conditional Pattern Routing for Budgeted Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29058)
*Chao Zhang,Yifan Ji,Ziyan Zhang,Kai Song,Fei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RouteSparse introduces a dynamic sparse attention method that routes each attention head and prompt segment to a small library of GPU-efficient sparse patterns. It uses a low-cost probe to estimate pattern utility and uncertainty, then selects an optimal pattern and sparsity budget via a latency-aware router, falling back to denser masks when uncertain. The routing is formulated as constrained risk minimization with an error certificate based on omitted probability mass. Evaluated on long-context tasks with Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct, RouteSparse achieves 6.5× speedup over dense prefill with only a 0.2-point RULER drop, outperforming fixed per-head routing (7.3× speedup, 1.6-point drop). Ablations show input-conditional routing, hardware profiling, and selective dense fallback improve the quality-latency tradeoff.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing dynamic sparse attention methods like MInference assign fixed sparse patterns to each attention head, assuming these patterns remain effective across diverse inputs. However, this assumption fails in practice, leading to suboptimal performance. The motivation is to develop a more adaptive, input-aware routing mechanism that can dynamically select efficient sparse patterns based on prompt content and hardware constraints.

Method: RouteSparse employs a small library of GPU-optimized sparse patterns. For each head and prompt segment, it uses a lightweight probe to estimate pattern utility and uncertainty. A latency-aware router then selects the best pattern and sparsity budget, with uncertain cases defaulting to denser masks. Routing is framed as constrained risk minimization, using an attention-output error certificate derived from omitted probability mass to guide decisions.

Result: On Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct with 128K-token prompts, RouteSparse achieves 6.5× faster prefill than dense attention while incurring only a 0.2-point RULER degradation. In comparison, fixed per-head routing yields 7.3× speedup but with a 1.6-point drop in RULER. Ablation studies confirm that input-conditional routing, hardware-aware pattern selection, and selective dense fallback are key contributors to improved performance-latency balance.

Conclusion: RouteSparse improves upon static sparse attention by enabling adaptive, input-dependent pattern selection through a low-cost, hardware-aware routing system. It achieves significant speedups with minimal accuracy loss, demonstrating that dynamic, context-sensitive routing enhances efficiency without sacrificing model fidelity.

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention can reduce the quadratic cost of long-context prefilling without changing model weights. MInference assigns each attention head one pattern offline and estimates that pattern's sparse indices for every prompt. This design is efficient, but it assumes that a head's preferred pattern and sparsity budget remain suitable across inputs. We introduce RouteSparse, which routes each head and prompt segment among a small library of GPU-efficient sparse patterns. A low-cost probe estimates pattern utility and uncertainty; a latency-aware router then selects a pattern and budget, while uncertain cases fall back to a denser mask. We formulate routing as constrained risk minimization, derive an attention-output error certificate from omitted probability mass, and evaluate the method on long-context retrieval, question answering, summarization, and language modeling. On Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct with 128K-token prompts, RouteSparse achieves $6.5\times$ dense prefill speed with a 0.2-point RULER drop relative to dense attention, compared with $7.3\times$ speed and a 1.6-point drop for fixed per-head routing. Ablations confirm that input-conditional routing, hardware profiling, and selective dense fallback each contribute to the quality--latency tradeoff.

</details>


### [80] [Not All or None: Dynamic Construction of Target-aware Memory Graph for Conversational Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29066)
*Yifan Xiang,Bin Liang,Yuqi Huang,Ruifeng Xu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TamGraph的新方法，用于对话立场检测，通过目标感知的记忆图动态利用相关历史语句，采用熵引导的逐步回溯机制选择性激活记忆，构建目标感知图来建模话语间的立场关系，从而在保持信息相关性的同时避免噪声干扰。实验表明该方法显著提升了大语言模型在对话立场检测任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话立场检测在真实社交媒体场景中更具挑战性，需要利用跨会话的目标相关历史陈述来识别用户立场，而现有方法或使用全部历史对话（引入噪声），或完全忽略历史信息，因此亟需一种能有效筛选并利用目标相关历史信息的方法。

Method: 提出TamGraph方法，采用步进式、熵引导的回溯机制，动态选择性激活历史对话中的目标相关语句，并构建目标感知的记忆图，以建模话语间的立场关系，实现对立场的精准捕捉。

Result: 在英文和中文基准数据集上的实验结果表明，TamGraph显著提升了大语言模型在对话立场检测任务上的表现，证明了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TamGraph通过智能选择历史信息并构建目标感知图，有效解决了对话立场检测中噪声干扰与信息遗漏的问题，为该任务提供了一种高效且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Stance detection is crucial for understanding the underlying attitude of an expression towards a target. Conversational stance detection is a more challenging stance detection task in real-world social media scenarios, as it involves detecting the user's stance by leveraging the target-related historical statements across conversational sessions. In this paper, we propose target-aware Memory Graph TamGraph, a novel method that dynamically leverages target-related statements for conversational stance detection. Instead of considering all preceding historical conversations or using no prior conversation information for stance detection, our TamGraph employs a stepwise, entropy-guided backtracking mechanism to selectively activate memory from historical conversations and dynamically constructs a target-aware graph to model the stance relations among utterances. This allows the exploitation of target-related information from the conversation history for stance detection while preventing the introduction of noise. Experimental results on both English and Chinese benchmarks demonstrate that our TamGraph substantially improves LLM performance on conversational stance detection.

</details>


### [81] [Recognition-Refusal Misalignment in LLMs: Why Models Answer Structurally Unanswerable Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29109)
*Yucheng Du,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现大型语言模型在面对结构上无法回答的问题（如计算cot(-540°)）时，会错误地给出答案而非拒绝回答。尽管模型在隐藏状态中已能识别此类问题的不可解性，但其识别信号与安全拒答路径几乎正交，表明失败源于路由机制而非识别能力缺失。通过生成阶段的定向调控，可有效改变模型对无效输入的行为，证明存在一个可用的“无合法答案”信号，但未被现有安全机制所利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型为何在面对结构上不可能的问题时仍强行作答，是由于缺乏识别能力还是识别后未能正确引导至拒绝回答的路径？

Method: 分析不同参数规模（1.7B至70B）的指令微调模型，通过隐藏状态空间中的方向分析，识别模型对结构不可解问题的表征；比较识别方向与安全拒答方向之间的几何关系；进行生成时间的定向干预实验以验证可调控性；对比基础模型与指令微调模型的预训练阶段表现。

Result: 模型在隐藏状态中已存在一个线性方向用于区分可回答与结构上不可能的问题，表明其具备识别能力；该识别方向与标准安全拒答方向近乎正交；基于任务定义的无效性感知方向虽更接近识别方向，但仍不完全对齐；生成阶段沿识别方向的干预能双向、剂量响应地改变模型行为，而随机方向无效；该低余弦几何关系在预训练阶段即已存在。

Conclusion: 模型对结构不可解问题的错误回答主要源于路由失败，而非编码或识别缺陷：模型拥有有效的‘无合法答案’信号，但当前的安全拒答机制并未对齐这一信号，导致其无法被正确激活。

Abstract: Large language models often answer structurally unanswerable questions, such as computing cot(-540°) or evaluating (1).startswith("1"), instead of abstaining. We ask whether this failure reflects missing recognition or failed routing from recognition to abstention. Across instruction-tuned models from 1.7B to 70B parameters, a single linear direction in the hidden state separates answerable from structurally impossible math and code prompts, showing that models represent impossibility before generation. Yet this recognition direction is nearly orthogonal to the canonical safety-refusal direction that mediates trained harmful-content refusal. An in-domain behavior-defined invalidity-aware direction is closer to recognition, but only partially aligned with it, and remains near-orthogonal to safety refusal. Generation-time steering along the recognition direction changes invalidity-aware behavior bidirectionally and dose-responsively on structural math and code cells, while random directions do not. Base/instruct comparisons further show that the low-cosine geometry is already present at the pretraining endpoint. The confident-on-impossible failure is therefore better explained as a routing failure than as an encoding failure: the model has a usable "no admissible answer" signal, but the safety-refusal pathway is not aligned to use it.

</details>


### [82] [HEAR Who Said What: Unlocking Speaker-Attributed Reasoning via Counterfactual Voice Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29120)
*Dongwook Lee,Sangkwon Park,Eunwoo Song,Che Hyun Lee,Youngho Cho,Junho Kim,June Young Yi,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEAR基准，用于评估语音语言模型在多说话人环境中的说话人属性推理能力。实验发现现有20个主流SLM在该任务上表现不佳，主要依赖语义先验而非声学线索。为此，作者提出A2R模型，基于对抗性音频与说话人级硬负样本（CASH）数据集进行优化，显著提升模型对声学特征的敏感度。A2R在HEAR上表现优异，并展现出零样本泛化能力，证明了良好的说话人属性识别可激活模型潜在的说话人感知推理能力。所有资源公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音语言模型在多说话人场景中难以准确进行说话人归属和推理，缺乏可靠评估工具和有效训练方法，亟需系统诊断与改进机制。

Method: 构建包含2.4K人工验证样本的HEAR基准，涵盖887段多样化多说话人音频；评估20个主流SLM；设计CASH数据集，通过反事实音频与说话人级硬负样本引导模型关注声学线索；训练30B参数的A2R模型以提升说话人属性推理能力。

Result: 多数SLMs在HEAR上表现差，依赖语义而非声学线索；A2R在HEAR上取得显著进步，并在多个下游任务中实现零样本泛化，表明其具备强大的说话人感知推理潜力。

Conclusion: 通过引入HEAR基准和A2R模型，研究揭示了现有SLMs在说话人属性推理方面的局限性，并证明通过针对性训练可有效激发模型的说话人感知推理能力，为未来多说话人语音理解系统的发展提供了新路径。

Abstract: Speech Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-speaker environments, yet their ability to attribute speech to the correct speaker and reason over speaker identities remains unclear. Hence, we introduce HEAR, a conceptually hierarchical benchmark diagnosing the foundational capabilities of speaker-attributed reasoning, comprising 2.4K human-verified samples from 887 diverse multi-party audio clips. Evaluating 20 leading SLMs on HEAR reveals they struggle with these foundational tasks, often relying on semantic priors rather than actual vocal cues. To address this, we present A2R, a 30B model optimized on Counterfactual Audio with Speaker-level Hard negatives (CASH), a dataset designed to guide the model to prioritize acoustic vocal cues over linguistic signals. A2R achieves strong performance on HEAR and exhibits zero-shot generalization to diverse multi-speaker downstream tasks, demonstrating that learned speaker attribution unlocks the model's latent capacity for speaker-aware reasoning. All resources are available at https://attributetoreason.github.io/AttributeToReason/

</details>


### [83] [AI Historian: Helping historians organize and verify person-centred temporal clues from dispersed historical narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29133)
*Yifeng Lu,Zijie Yang,Jie Li,Qingkai Min,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AI Historian (AIH) is an AI agent system that helps historians organize scattered biographical and temporal evidence from historical texts. It identifies people, temporal cues, verifies cross-text associations, and infers comparable time ranges while preserving traceable source evidence. Evaluated on Shiji cases, AIH achieved 86.2% MicroIoU in temporal localization—surpassing human-only annotation (81.3%) and direct LLM prompting (17.1%)—and reduced processing time from 1h32min to 14min. Applied across Chinese, Japanese, Korean, and modern histories, results are publicly available via Westlake Historian, enabling scalable, traceable, and collaborative historical research.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Historical narratives are fragmented across texts and chapters, making it difficult for historians to reconstruct accurate timelines and verify relationships. Existing methods lack systematic, traceable, and scalable approaches to organizing person-time evidence.

Method: AIH processes source sentences as evidence units, uses NLP to detect people and temporal cues, verifies cross-text associations, and infers consistent temporal ranges while maintaining traceability to original sources.

Result: AIH achieved 86.2% MicroIoU in temporal localization—outperforming both human-only annotation (81.3%) and direct LLM prompting (17.1%). Processing time was reduced from 1h32min to just 14min. The system has been successfully applied to multiple historical corpora, including the Twenty-Four Histories and ancient East Asian records.

Conclusion: AIH enables efficient, scalable, and transparent organization of historical evidence, transforming obscure narrative connections into testable, collaborative research questions, thereby advancing large-scale historical analysis.

Abstract: History is not preserved in complete, continuous form. Accounts of a person's activities, relationships and historical contexts are scattered across texts, chapters and narrative perspectives; historians must retrieve, identify and compare these materials to reconstruct temporal sequences and verify them against sources. Here we present AI Historian (AIH), an AI agent system that helps historians organize person-time evidence from dispersed biographical narratives. It takes source sentences as evidence units, identifies people and temporal cues, verifies candidate cross-text associations and infers comparable temporal ranges while preserving traceable source-text evidence. We evaluated AIH on six Shiji cases concerning Liu Bang, Xiang Yu and Xiao He. AIH Agent achieved a temporal-localization MicroIoU of 86.2%, compared with 81.3% for human-only annotation and 17.1% for direct large-language-model prompting; it required about 14 min, versus 1 h 32 min for human-only annotation. We further applied AIH to the Twenty-Four Histories and other ancient Chinese histories, ancient Japanese and Korean histories, and modern and contemporary historical materials, and released the results through Westlake Historian. These results indicate that AIH can reduce the cost of organizing historical materials at scale while turning connections obscured by chapter-based narration into traceable, revisable research questions for collaborative testing.

</details>


### [84] [Quantifying Error Tolerance in Synthetic Data: An Atomic-level Operand vs. Operator Perturbation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29144)
*Jiaxiang Liu,Chenhao Yuan,Shuwen Xu,Boxuan Xing,Xiusheng Huang,Yinhao Xu,Hao Liu,Wenhao Teng,Xiangwen Liao,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOM框架，通过将数据分解为功能单元（f(x)→y），区分良性操作数（x）扰动与致命操作符（f）扰动，解决现有过滤策略过于激进或宽松的问题。实验发现模型对操作数扰动鲁棒，但对操作符扰动敏感，强调操作符多样性比操作数精度更重要，基于ATOM生成的数据在性能上优于基线（如比LIMA提升3.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前合成数据生成缺乏对错误容忍度的量化分析，导致过滤策略要么过于严格排除有价值样本，要么过于宽松无法有效剔除错误样本，阻碍大语言模型发展。

Method: 提出原子树操作建模（ATOM）框架，将数据分解为函数形式 f(x)→y，区分操作数与操作符的扰动类型，并据此设计更精准的过滤策略。

Result: 模型对操作数扰动具有鲁棒性，但对操作符扰动极为敏感；使用ATOM合成的数据在性能上显著优于现有基线方法，例如相比LIMA提升3.1%。

Conclusion: 操作符多样性在合成数据中比操作数精度更为关键，ATOM框架有效提升了数据质量与模型性能。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation has become a cornerstone for advancing large language models. However, the lack of the quantitative analysis for error tolerance became a critical bottleneck. Consequently, current filtering strategies fluctuate between two extremes: they are either overly aggressive, risking the exclusion of potentially valuable samples, or overly permissive, failing to eliminate erroneous samples effectively. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces Atomic Tree Operation Modeling (ATOM), a framework that decomposes data into functional units ($f(x)\rightarrow y$). ATOM distinguishes benign Operand $x$ perturbations from fatal Operator $f$ perturbations. The former are needlessly discarded by aggressive filtering, while the latter slip through permissive filtering. Our experiments reveal a double dissociation: models are robust to operand perturbations but collapse under operator perturbations. By prioritizing operator over aggressive operand precision, our ATOM-synthesized data outperforms rigorous baselines (e.g., +3.1% gain over LIMA), suggesting that operator diversity matters more than operand precision. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lut-hub/ATOM.

</details>


### [85] [Toward a Cross-Lingual Romanization Ecosystem for Sinitic Languages: A Paired Mandarin-Cantonese Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29170)
*Zijie Zhang,Tan Lee,Yong Cao,Benyou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Sinitic罗马化生态系统，一个跨语言的Sinitic罗马化设计框架，包含数字基础设施和社区驱动的开源工作流。该框架通过四个设计原则（语音对应、历史音位对应、一音素一符号、基本拉丁字母使用）解决Sinitic语言间罗马化对齐缺乏系统性的问题，并在粤语和普通话中开发了CantRomZJ1和MandRomZJ1方案，同时扩展至梅县客家话、上海吴语和南京江淮官话。构建了支持结构化存储、转换、解析、词典构建和输入法生成的开源基础设施，并通过Meta的多语言语音微调数据集评估，结果显示相比拼音+粤拼基线，新方案在粤语的词错误率（WER）和字符错误率（CER）分别降低7.80%和10.61%，表明跨语言罗马化对齐可提升低资源Sinitic语音技术的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sinitic语言罗马化系统缺乏跨语言一致性，导致不同语言间难以实现有效的语音技术迁移与共享，亟需一套系统化、统一的罗马化设计框架以促进跨语言信息处理。

Method: 提出基于语音对应、历史音位对应、一音素一符号、基本拉丁字母使用的四原则设计框架；开发CantRomZJ1（粤语）、MandRomZJ1（普通话）及其它方言罗马化方案；构建开源基础设施支持存储、转换、解析、词典构建与输入法生成；通过MMS语音模型进行语音到罗马化的实验评估。

Result: 在语音转罗马化任务中，采用MandRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1的条件相比Pinyin+Jyutping基线，粤语的词错误率（WER）下降7.80%，字符错误率（CER）下降10.61%，证明跨语言罗马化对齐能有效提升低资源语音技术的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 跨语言罗马化对齐是提升Sinitic语言语音技术迁移性能的关键，本研究提出的框架及其配套基础设施为多语言Sinitic语音处理提供了可扩展、可复用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Sinitic Romanization Ecosystem, a cross-lingual Sinitic romanization design framework with supporting digital infrastructure and a community-driven open-source workflow. The design framework addresses the lack of systematic cross-lingual romanization alignment among Sinitic languages through four design principles: phonetic correspondence for representing similar sounds with similar romanized symbols, historical-phonological correspondence for aligning cognate romanization strings, one-phoneme-one-symbol, and basic Latin-letter use, with a balancing consideration recognizing trade-offs among these principles. For the main paired case study, we devel-op CantRomZJ1 and MandRomZJ1, Cantonese and Manda-rin romanization schemes following the design framework, respectively. We also develop schemes for several other Sinitic languages, including Meixian Hakka, Shanghai Wu, and Nanjing Jianghuai Mandarin, following the same de-sign framework. To bring the romanization schemes into practical use, we develop open-source infrastructure for structured romanization storage, conversion, parsing, dic-tionary construction, and input-method generation. Finally, we evaluate the design framework through speech-to-romanization experiments based on Meta's Massively Mul-tilingual Speech (MMS) fine-tuning. Compared with the Pinyin+Jyutping baseline, our Man-dRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1 condition reduces Cantonese WER and CER by 7.80% and 10.61%, respectively. These results suggest that cross-lingual romanization alignment can improve transfer in low-resource Sinitic speech technology.

</details>


### [86] [Toward Cultural Alignment: Human-Centered Evaluation of Multimodal AI Stories Across Five African Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29209)
*Millicent Ochieng,Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Elizabeth A. Ankrah,Najeeb Gambo Abdulhamid,Migisha Boyd,Stephanie Nyairo,Mercy Muchai,Samuel Chege Maina,Aditya Vashistha,Anja Thieme,Jacki O'Neill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了AI生成的多模态故事在非洲五个社区中的文化契合度，发现文化契合不仅依赖于可见的文化符号，更取决于这些符号在社会、语言、程序和视觉语境中的适配性。研究提出了一个包含五类文化标记和八种常见错配机制的文化契合分类体系，并评估了五种多模态大模型评判工具的表现，发现其可靠性与校准度在不同社区间差异显著，无一工具在所有场景中表现一致。因此，研究提倡建立基于社区校准的评估流程，通过社区判断验证自动化评判工具的可信度，明确其适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI生成内容在跨文化情境下的真实性与文化契合度，避免因机械复制文化符号而造成误解或冒犯，尤其在非洲多元文化背景下确保技术应用的伦理与有效性。

Method: 采用混合方法，结合定量标注与定性焦点小组讨论，对19位来自五个非洲社区的文化代表进行调研，系统分析文化符号在多模态叙事中的呈现与接受情况，并构建文化契合分类体系；同时测试五种多模态大模型评判工具在不同社区中的表现。

Result: 文化契合度高度依赖于上下文整合而非孤立的文化符号；提出五类文化标记与八种错配机制；五种自动化评判工具在不同社区表现不一，缺乏普遍可靠性，需以社区判断为基准进行校准。

Conclusion: 应发展社区校准的自动化评估框架，将人类社区判断作为黄金标准，以确定自动化工具的应用范围，确保多模态AI内容在跨文化传播中的准确性和尊重性。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine how well AI-generated multimodal stories align with the lived practices, relationships, language, values, and visual expectations of the communities they represent. We conduct a community-grounded mixed-methods evaluation with 19 culture representatives across five African communities, combining quantitative annotations with qualitative focus group discussions. We find that cultural alignment depends not simply on recognizable cultural markers, but on how those markers fit social, linguistic, procedural, and visual context. From these evaluations, we develop a taxonomy of cultural alignment comprising five broader cultural marker categories and eight recurring mechanisms of misalignment. We additionally evaluate five multimodal LLM judges to examine whether automated evaluation can approximate community-grounded judgments at scale. Judge reliability and score calibration vary substantially across communities, with no single judge performing consistently across all five settings. These findings motivate community-calibrated evaluation pipelines in which automated judges are validated against community judgments to determine where they can be trusted and where human review remains necessary.

</details>


### [87] [Attribute-Based Activation Steering of LLMs for Group-Specific Explanation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29215)
*Leandra Fichtel,Janek Prange,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何利用大语言模型（LLM）生成针对特定人群的个性化解释。提出一种基于激活工程的方法，通过识别目标群体的解释风格和知识特征，计算属性导向的引导向量，并在推理过程中注入模型内部激活以实现细粒度控制。实验表明，该方法在解释的针对性和事实性方面优于提示法和现有先进引导基线，且在人类专家评估中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仅靠提示难以生成符合特定人群背景和能力的个性化解释，缺乏有效的计算方法来实现这一目标，因此需要探索如何让大语言模型更好地适应不同受众的需求。

Method: 首先识别目标群体的解释风格与知识特征；基于激活工程计算属性相关的引导向量；在推理阶段将这些向量注入到LLM的内部激活中，实现对输出内容的细粒度引导。

Result: 所提方法在解释的针对性和事实性上显著优于提示法及现有先进引导方法，在人类专家评估中也表现出更强的适配性。

Conclusion: 通过属性导向的激活引导，大语言模型能够有效生成更贴合特定群体需求的解释，为个性化知识传播提供了新路径。

Abstract: To effectively enable people to understand new topics, explanations should be tailored to their backgrounds and abilities. So far, prompting alone has been shown to be insufficient for creating such explanations and other computational methods are missing. Therefore, this paper investigates whether LLMs can be steered to generate explanations that are tailored to a specific group of people. To this end, we propose an approach that first identifies group-specific attributes in terms of explanatory style and knowledge of a specific target group. Building on activation engineering, it then computes attribute-based steering vectors and adds them to the internal activations of an LLM during inference to enable a fine-grained steering. In our experiments, we assess the steering effectiveness of our approach in terms of specificity and factuality of the generated explanations. Additionally, we evaluate the explanations in a study with human experts from different target groups. Compared to prompting and state-of-the-art steering baselines, our approach tailors the explanations significantly better to the target group while largely maintaining factuality.

</details>


### [88] [Anchoring Speech with Semantics: A Multimodal Adapter Mechanism for Automatic Speech Recognition in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29239)
*Kuan-Tang Huang,Cheng-Yeh Yang,Chien-Chun Wang,Hung-Shin Lee,Hsin-Min Wang,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAMA-ASR提出一种轻量级适配器机制，通过引入辅助翻译的语义锚和语音锚来增强解码器，在低资源语音识别中结合语义与声学信息以提升性能。该方法可在无需人工标注翻译的情况下自动生成语义锚，实验表明其在台湾闽南语和客家话等低资源方言上优于现有基线模型，且小型语音到文本转换模型即可生成有效语义锚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语音识别因训练文本稀缺而面临监督信号不足的问题，现有方法难以有效利用外部语义信息。为弥补这一缺陷，需一种能融合多模态信息（如翻译语义和语音特征）的轻量级机制来增强解码过程。

Method: SAMA-ASR引入语义锚（来自辅助翻译）和声学锚（来自语音），通过跨模态适应将解码状态同时关联于翻译生成的语义嵌入和原始语音嵌入，实现对句子级语义与语音证据的联合建模；评估时语义锚可由上游语音到文本翻译模型自动生成，无需人工提供。

Result: 在两个30小时的低资源汉藏方言数据集（台湾闽南语、客家话）上，SAMA-ASR显著优于仅使用声学、提示引导或纯语义引导的基线模型，且在实际自动语义锚设置下仍保持有效性；分析显示，小型语音翻译模型即可生成有用的语义锚。

Conclusion: SAMA-ASR是一种高效、可扩展的低资源语音识别框架，能够通过自动生成的语义锚融合多模态信息，有效缓解数据稀缺带来的挑战，适用于多种编码器-解码器多任务语音模型。

Abstract: Low-resource ASR remains difficult because scarce transcripts provide limited supervised evidence for target-side generation. To address this gap, we propose SAMA-ASR, a lightweight adapter mechanism that augments the decoder with semantic anchors from auxiliary translations and an acoustic anchor from speech; in principle, the mechanism can be applied to similar encoder--decoder multitask speech models. Through cross-modal adaptation, SAMA-ASR conditions decoder states on translation-derived semantic embeddings and a speech embedding, combining utterance-level meaning with speech-grounded evidence before token prediction. At evaluation time, these semantic anchors can be generated automatically by an upstream speech-to-text translator rather than supplied as oracle translations. Experiments on two 30-hour datasets covering the low-resource Sinitic varieties Taiwanese Hokkien and Hakka show that SAMA-ASR improves over acoustic, prior prompt-based, and semantic-only translation-guided baselines and remains effective in practical automatic semantic-anchor settings; translator-capacity analyses show that useful semantic anchors can be produced by a compact ST model.

</details>


### [89] [When Patients Cut In: Extending Clinical Conversational AI Safety to Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29241)
*Zachary Ellis,Spencer Hazel,Adam Brandt,Yajie Vera He,Ernest Lim,Jared Joselowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究临床语音助手在患者打断情况下的内容恢复能力，提出基于对话分析的中断类型分类（识别性、竞争性、过渡性子单元），评估四种非推理型大模型在信息收集与信息提供场景中的表现。结果显示，不同模型在不同场景下表现差异显著，尤其在竞争性打断时所有模型均出现30/30的内容遗漏；道歉语句虽可提升部分模型表现，但效果不一致甚至适得其反。因此，中断鲁棒性需按任务场景和中断类型分别评估，不能用单一指标衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语音助手在真实医疗环境中常遭遇患者打断，而现有评估基准多假设患者等待完成发言，忽略打断导致的关键信息丢失问题，亟需建立更贴近实际的评估方法。

Method: 将对话分析中的重叠类型转化为三种可操作的中断类型，设计涵盖历史采集与常见问题解答的四组实验场景，测试四种非推理型大语言模型在不同中断情境下的内容保留能力，并引入简短道歉提示观察影响。

Result: 在信息收集任务中，各模型目标问题失败率各异；在信息提供任务中，所有模型失败率上升；竞争性打断下所有模型在问答场景中均出现完全内容遗漏（30/30失败）；道歉提示对恢复能力有显著但不一致的影响，个别模型反而下降。

Conclusion: 中断鲁棒性无法用单一分数衡量，评估必须基于具体任务内容、中断类型和部署场景进行细分报告，以真实反映系统在临床交互中的可靠性。

Abstract: Clinical voice agents are now deployed in routine care, where real patients do not wait their turn: they interrupt. These systems typically use a cascaded architecture (speech-to-text -> LLM -> text-to-speech), so when a patient cuts the agent off mid-utterance, clinically required content can be lost even when the model handles cooperative transcripts well. Yet clinical conversational-AI benchmarks almost universally assume patients wait for the agent to finish, missing interruption-induced loss of required content. We present a transcript-based evaluation of interruption recovery, adapting conversation-analytic overlap categories into three operational types (recognitional, competitive, transitional sub-unit) and testing four deployment-oriented, non-reasoning LLM configurations across four cells spanning history-taking (information gathering) and FAQ (information provision), scored on whether the agent preserves the clinically required content. In the gathering cells, target-question failure varied across models; in the provision cells, where arms are directly comparable, failure rose for every model. Rankings differ across cells, and competitive FAQ interruption produced 30/30 provision-coverage failures for all four models (Wilson 95% CI: 88.6-100.0%; baseline 0/30 for three, 4/30 for Llama). A brief apology marker ("sorry to interrupt") shifts recovery by tens of percentage points, inconsistently across models, and for one it reduces recovery. Interruption robustness therefore cannot be a single score: evaluation must be content-grounded, reported per cell, and matched to the deployment's interruption profile.

</details>


### [90] [Large Language Models Systematically Favor Popular Options: Evidence and Mitigation Across MCQs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29257)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Mohammed Ali,Bhawna Piryani,Mahmoud Abdalla,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为PopMCQ的基准测试，用于系统性地评估大语言模型在多选题中的流行度偏差问题。研究表明，现代LLMs倾向于选择更受欢迎但错误的答案，即使其置信度很高。为此，作者提出了PopDebias方法，在推理阶段通过去除流行度先验来纠正这一偏差，无需微调，仅需少量校准数据，计算开销极小。实验在22个开源LLM上验证了该方法的有效性，准确率提升最高达54.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在多选题评估中存在对答案选项流行度的依赖，导致模型偏好高流行度但错误的选项，从而影响评估的公平性和准确性。这种现象被称为‘流行度偏差’，亟需一个可控的基准和有效的缓解方法。

Method: 引入PopMCQ基准，设计六种控制策略，通过改变选项流行度而固定正确答案，以系统性地分析流行度偏差；提出PopDebias方法，在推理阶段估计并移除模型预测中的流行度先验，实现无监督、轻量级的去偏。

Result: 在最极端的设置下，模型选择流行但错误答案的比例高达66%；使用PopDebias后，22个不同规模的LLM（0.5B至32B参数）均表现出显著准确率提升，最高达54.1个百分点，且计算成本极低。

Conclusion: 流行度偏差是当前大语言模型评估中不可忽视的问题，而PopDebias提供了一种高效、通用、无需微调的解决方案，显著提升了模型在多选题任务中的可靠性与公平性。

Abstract: Multiple-choice questions (MCQs) are a standard format for evaluating large language models (LLMs), yet the popularity of answer options can confound evaluation. Modern LLMs systematically prefer popular but incorrect options over less popular correct ones, a vulnerability we call \textbf{popularity bias}. This pattern aligns with confidence miscalibration: model confidence remains high even as accuracy collapses for popular options. To systematically isolate this phenomenon, we introduce \textbf{PopMCQ}, a benchmark with six controlled strategies that vary option popularity while keeping the correct answer fixed. In our most adversarial setting, where all distractors are more popular than the correct option, models choose popular but wrong answers 66\% of the time. To mitigate this bias, we propose \textbf{PopDebias}, a lightweight inference-time correction that estimates and removes a popularity prior from model predictions. It requires no fine-tuning, is label-free at test time (using only a small calibration split for parameter fitting), and adds negligible computational cost. Experiments on 22 open-source LLMs (0.5B to 32B parameters) show consistent improvements, with accuracy gains up to 54.1 percentage points under strong popularity pressure. The code and data are available https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/PopMCQ

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [ERR+: Sequential Entropy Resolution for Efficient and Decisive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28771)
*Xin Jiang,Minhao Wang,Wen Wu,Zhentao Xie,Shangheng Du,Jinxin Shi,Jiabao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ERR+，一种基于令牌级熵下降的两阶段强化学习框架，用于优化大模型在复杂任务中的推理过程。第一阶段使用熵缓解奖励（ERR），通过奖励推理过程中熵的累积下降来引导高质量推理；第二阶段引入稳健相对效率奖励，通过比较生成响应与同伴响应的长度，提升简洁性。实验表明，该方法在多个数据集上均提升了准确率和响应简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法虽能提高正确性，但对推理过程质量缺乏有效指导，尤其在内部推理结构优化方面表现不足。本文通过分析发现，正确的推理轨迹通常表现出更频繁且更大的令牌级熵下降，这为改进推理质量提供了新线索。

Method: 提出两阶段RLVR框架ERR+：第一阶段采用熵缓解奖励（ERR），奖励推理中累积的令牌级熵下降，并按响应长度进行对数归一化；第二阶段采用稳健相对效率奖励，通过双曲正切变换的组内z-score比较响应长度，鼓励简洁高效输出。同时，形式化分析揭示了两个目标在早期训练中的梯度冲突，支持分阶段设计。

Result: 在五个不同数据集上的实验显示，ERR+在多种模型架构下均显著提升推理准确率和响应简洁性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ERR+通过结合熵下降信号与相对效率评估，有效引导推理过程的质量与效率，为大模型的链式思考优化提供了新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve strong performance on complex tasks by generating extended chain-of-thought (CoT) traces via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). While current RLVR methods have achieved strong results with correctness-based reward signals, they provide limited guidance on the quality of the reasoning process itself, leaving the internal reasoning structure largely unoptimized. Through empirical analysis across multiple model families, we identify a consistent pattern: correct reasoning trac es exhibit more frequent and larger token-level entropy drops within the thinking phase than incorrect ones. We propose ERR+, a two-phase RLVR framework grounded in this observation. The first phase trains with the Entropy Relief Reward (ERR), a bonus proportional to cumulative token-level entropy drops in the thinking phase, log-normalized by response length. Unlike prior methods that suppress entropy, ERR rewards the resolution of uncertainty while leaving exploratory high-entropy states unconstrained. The second phase introduces the Robust Relative Efficiency Reward, which scores each response's length against co-generated peers via a $\tanh$-transformed within-group $z$-score. We provide a formal analysis showing that joint optimization of the two objectives induces gradient conflict in early training, motivating the sequential design . Experiments on five datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in both accuracy and response conciseness across model backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/XrkArul/err_response

</details>


### [92] [Unsupervised Latent Space Alignment with Hyperspherical Geodesic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28840)
*Cameron Ryan,Vivek Sivaraman Narayanaswamy,Kowshik Thopalli,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HGA（超球面高斯对齐）方法，通过最大化潜在空间间的几何匹配度来实现无监督或弱监督下的跨模型对齐，无需依赖成对样本对应关系，可在模型拼接和多语言词嵌入映射任务中达到与有监督方法相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖于共享样本对应关系（锚点）进行潜在空间对齐，但这类方法受限于标注数据的可用性。本文旨在探索是否仅凭不同潜在空间中相似数据的几何特征就足以实现对齐，从而减少对标注数据的依赖。

Method: 提出HGA方法，基于潜在空间的几何结构，通过优化一个变换以最大化两个潜在空间之间的几何适配度，利用超球面上的高斯分布建模潜在表示的分布特性，并直接在几何层面进行对齐。

Result: 在模型拼接和多语言词嵌入对应恢复等任务上，HGA在极少或无监督条件下达到了与有监督方法相当的性能，验证了仅通过几何签名即可实现有效对齐的可行性。

Conclusion: 几何结构本身蕴含足够的信息用于潜在空间对齐，无需依赖成对样本，HGA为无监督和弱监督下的跨模型对齐提供了高效且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks tend to encode the same data with similar latent geometries. These latent geometries are not directly compatible, yet they can be nearly the same up to some class of transformations. While there exists many methods for alignment between different latent spaces, it is typically done using a set of shared sample correspondences, known as anchors. This leaves a fundamental question: are the geometric signatures of different latent spaces representing similar data sufficient to recover an alignment between them? To that end, we introduce HGA (Hyperspherical Gaussian Alignment), a method that directly optimizes a transformation between two latent spaces by maximizing a geometric measure of "fit" between them. Since it is driven by the geometry of the latent spaces rather than paired data, HGA can operate in both an unsupervised and weakly supervised regime. On tasks such as model stitching or multilingual word embedding correspondence recovery, HGA manages to match supervised results with minimal or no supervision.

</details>


### [93] [Curvature Cryptanalysis of Smooth Transformer Feed-Forward Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28843)
*Munawar Hasan,Apostol Vassilev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了平滑的两层前馈网络（FFN）在选定输入原始输出查询访问下存在额外的结构模型提取通道，利用二阶泄漏信息（投影输入海森矩阵混合相同的隐藏对称秩一因子），通过部分对称分解建立局部可识别性和稳定性条件，并通过向量输出模板复用将结构查询成本降低16倍。在独立训练的CIFAR-10视觉变换器上，仅需16个投影海森矩阵（对应8193次黑盒查询），即可以平均绝对余弦相似度超过0.94恢复隐藏的FFN方向；95.1%的GELU和91.9%的SiLU方向达到0.90以上对齐。该方法在不同模型、重复实验及所有变换器块中均表现稳定，且恢复的结构支持功能替代：固定方向并仅拟合其余参数，即可生成高保真替代模型，准确率与目标模型差距小于0.90%（GELU）和0.62%（SiLU）。输出舍入和高斯噪声虽会降低恢复效果，但调整有限差分步长后，平均对齐度仍可恢复至0.9603（GELU）和0.9398（SiLU）。本研究实现了从黑盒二阶观测到内部FFN结构恢复与功能替换的端到端路径，在给定查询模型下，平滑FFN曲率暴露了行为保真度无法揭示的内部参数几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒攻击主要依赖函数输出行为，难以揭示深层神经网络内部结构。本文旨在探索平滑前馈网络中隐藏的二阶几何信息是否可被用于结构提取，从而实现无需参数或梯度访问的模型逆向工程。

Method: 提出基于选定输入原始输出查询的二阶泄漏分析框架，利用投影海森矩阵的对称秩一结构进行部分对称分解，结合向量输出模板复用策略，降低查询成本；通过有限差分近似计算海森矩阵，设计适应性步长调整机制以应对噪声干扰。

Result: 在独立训练的视觉变换器上，仅用16个投影海森矩阵（8193次查询）即可实现平均0.94以上的余弦对齐度；95.1%（GELU）和91.9%（SiLU）方向对齐度超0.90；功能替代模型与原模型在准确率上差异极小（<0.90%和<0.62%）；噪声环境下通过步长自适应仍可恢复高对齐度（0.9603和0.9398）。

Conclusion: 平滑两层前馈网络在黑盒二阶观察下暴露了可被利用的内部结构信息，突破了传统行为级攻击的局限，实现了从曲率信息到完整结构与功能的逆向重建，为模型透明性与安全性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We show that smooth two-layer feed-forward networks (FFNs) expose an additional structural model extraction channel under a chosen-input raw-output oracle at the FFN branch; consider transformer FFN branches with GELU or SiLU activations under chosen-input raw-output access, without access to parameters, gradients, or internal activations; exploit a second-order leakage channel in which projected input Hessians form different mixtures of the same hidden symmetric rank-one factors induced by the FFN input weights. We formalize resulting Hessian collection as a partially symmetric decomposition to establish conditions for local identifiability and stability to exploit vector-output stencil reuse to reduce the structural query cost by a factor of 16. On independently trained CIFAR-10 vision transformers, only 16 projected Hessians, corresponding to 8193 black-box queries, recover the hidden FFN directions with average absolute cosine alignment above 0.94, with 95.1 % of GELU and 91.9 % of SiLU directions exceeding 0.90 alignment. Recovery remains high across independently trained models, repeated extraction runs, and all transformer blocks. The recovered structure supports functional extraction too. Keeping the recovered directions fixed and fitting only the remaining FFN parameters yields high-fidelity substitutes with more than 93 % top-1 agreement, while test accuracy remains within 0.90% and 0.62% of the GELU and SiLU targets. Output rounding and Gaussian noise substantially reduce recovery under a fixed attack configuration, but adapting the finite-difference step restores average alignment to 0.9603 and 0.9398. This is an end-to-end path from black-box second-order observations to hidden FFN-structure recovery and functional replacement. Under the stated oracle model, smooth FFN curvature exposes internal parameter geometry that behavioral fidelity alone cannot reveal.

</details>


### [94] [Equivariant Sheaf Neural Networks: Learning Geometric Transport on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28853)
*Alessio Borgi,Mario Severino,Fabrizio Silvestri,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ESNN introduces an Equivariant Sheaf Neural Network that enhances geometric modeling in graphs by learning matrix-valued, directed transport of vector features while preserving Euclidean equivariance. It maintains first-order representations but increases flexibility via edge transport, enabling richer, feature-conditioned transformations. Theoretical analysis shows decomposition into radial and tangential components under relative displacement, and it supports symmetry relaxation for systems with preferred directions. ESNN improves performance in particle dynamics, mesh simulation, point-cloud classification, and molecular prediction, offering better long-horizon predictions and robustness to unseen rotations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing equivariant graph neural networks struggle with limited transformation capabilities for vector information across edges, especially in first-order architectures. There is a need for more expressive yet efficient models that preserve geometric symmetries without increasing representation order.

Method: ESNN designs a sheaf-based message passing framework where edge transport is modeled as learnable, matrix-valued functions conditioned on geometric inputs. It ensures exact O(n)-equivariance and allows controlled relaxation of symmetry when a preferred ambient direction exists. The model leverages relative displacement and learned covariant features to enable complex, directional interactions between node vectors.

Result: ESNN achieves state-of-the-art results in particle dynamics prediction, recovers gravity axis in symmetry-broken systems, outperforms baselines on mesh tasks and long-horizon rollouts, and remains robust to unseen rotations. It demonstrates superior expressiveness without requiring higher-order representations.

Conclusion: Learning how geometric information is transported across edges provides a powerful, complementary mechanism for enhancing equivariant message passing. By focusing on edge-level transport rather than increasing representation order, ESNN achieves high expressiveness while preserving exact equivariance and generalizing well across diverse geometric tasks.

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks provide a principled way to model geometric systems, but efficient first-order architectures remain limited in how vector information can be transformed as it moves across a graph. We introduce \textsc{ESNN}, an Equivariant Sheaf Neural Network that enriches this interaction by learning directed, matrix-valued transport between neighboring vector features while preserving exact Euclidean equivariance. Rather than increasing the order of the representation, ESNN keeps scalar and vector features first-order and places the additional geometric flexibility in the edge transport itself. We characterize this transport theoretically, showing that when relative displacement is the only covariant geometric input, every linear $O(n)$-equivariant map decomposes into independent radial and tangential components, while learned covariant features enable richer feature-conditioned transformations. We also introduce controlled symmetry relaxation for systems with a preferred ambient direction, which may be prescribed or inferred from data while recovering full $E(n)$-equivariance when the directional pathway is inactive. Across particle dynamics, mesh-based simulation, point-cloud classification, and molecular property prediction, ESNN improves dynamics prediction, recovers the gravity axis when symmetry is broken, yields substantial gains on selected mesh tasks and long-horizon rollouts, and remains robust to unseen rotations. These results show that learning how geometric information is transported across edges offers a complementary route to expressive equivariant message passing without requiring higher-order representations.

</details>


### [95] [The Halt Vector: Internalizing a Causal Steering Intervention for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28859)
*Dylan Jayabahu,Tinuade Adeleke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过内化因果可解释性发现，利用层18的停止向量（halt vector）来控制模型推理长度，有效减少不必要的思维过程。该方法在不依赖强化学习的情况下，从24个问题中训练得出，在五个未见过的基准测试上平均减少约四分之一的推理时间，且能根据每个问题的可削减冗余动态调整，同时解决因难度增加导致的非终止病态问题。关键在于重构受控激活时固定非轴向维度，避免破坏下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型在已知答案后仍持续生成思维链，导致计算浪费；传统全局长度惩罚无法灵活适应不同问题的冗余程度，因此需要一种更精细、可自适应的机制来缩短推理长度。

Method: 在模型第18层引入一个停止向量（halt vector），通过控制其方向和强度来调节推理长度；为避免干扰其他重要特征，采用固定非轴向维度的方式重建激活值，确保模型输出质量不受影响。该方法无需强化学习，仅基于少量样本进行拟合。

Result: 在五个未见过的基准测试上，该方法平均减少约25%的推理长度，且与各问题可削减冗余的相关性达到0.70；有效缓解了随问题难度上升的非终止问题，并改善了基于置信度的提前退出策略带来的恶化现象。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献并非在效率-准确率权衡上超越现有方法，而是提出了一个通过内部化因果可解释性发现实现推理长度可控的新机制，具有可解释性和可迁移性，为高效推理模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reasoning models do not stop when they know the answer. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B the chain of thought runs about twice as long as the model's own answer probability takes to settle, and how much of that excess is removable varies from problem to problem, so a global length penalty cannot take it out. We take it out by internalizing a causal interpretability finding into the weights. The mechanism is a halt vector: a difference-of-means direction at layer 18 of this model whose steering strength controls how long it thinks, while a replicated value axis does nothing. Installing that intervention in the weights is harder than it looks. Maximizing the scalar projection onto the direction corrupts the off-axis dimensions a frozen downstream reader depends on, and generation gets longer instead of shorter; what works is reconstructing the whole steered activation with those dimensions pinned to their natural values. Fit from 24 problems and no reinforcement learning, the halt removes about a quarter of the thinking at held accuracy across five unseen benchmarks, and the cut tracks each problem's own removable slack at 0.70. It also closes a non-termination pathology that grows with difficulty and that a decoding-time confidence hook makes worse. We do not claim to beat a well-tuned length penalty or decoding-time early exit on the raw trade-off; the contribution is how the halt is obtained.

</details>


### [96] [Conservative Hybrid Graph Networks for Process Systems with Learned Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28896)
*Paolo Guida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHGN 是一种数据驱动的混合图网络，能够学习工业过程网络中的路由、运行状态分配和去除率，并将其嵌入到固定的传输方程中，确保质量平衡始终成立。该模型在10-20个节点的网络上训练后，无需重新训练即可零样本迁移至25-40个节点的未见图，性能显著优于传统GNN基线（RMSE 2.1e-3 vs. 6e-2–9e-2），并实现高精度的门控和运行状态预测（门控MAE 7.9e-3，状态准确率94.3%）。在真实流体混合实验装置上，模型能有效应对未见物理故障，但无法预测人工干预（因阀门操作未被观测）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程网络拓扑动态变化，传统图神经网络在建模时难以揭示隐藏的物理机制，且缺乏对质量守恒的显式保证，导致模型预测虽拟合轨迹但缺乏物理意义。因此需要一种既能捕捉动态结构又能保证物理一致性、并具备强泛化能力的模型。

Method: 提出保守混合图网络（CHGN），将路由、运行状态与去除率作为可学习的代理变量，嵌入固定形式的质量守恒传输方程中，使模型预测始终满足质量平衡约束。通过联合优化网络结构与物理规律，实现对复杂动态系统的精准建模与解释。

Result: CHGN在零样本迁移任务中表现优异，在25-40节点的新拓扑上达到RMSE 2.1e-3，显著优于基线模型（6e-2–9e-2）；门控预测误差为7.9e-3，运行状态识别准确率达94.3%。在实际工业场景中，能有效检测物理故障，但无法预测未被观测的人工干预行为。

Conclusion: CHGN通过将物理守恒定律嵌入学习框架，实现了对动态工业过程网络的高效建模与跨拓扑泛化，不仅提升了预测精度，还暴露了系统内部的潜在运行机制，为可解释性分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Industrial process networks do not maintain a single effective topology while operating: streams are throttled or bypassed, and units move between idle, transition, and active regimes. Models of such systems are typically trained on measured state trajectories while the operating mechanisms that generated them remain latent, and an unconstrained graph network can fit such a trajectory without assigning stable physical meaning to the recovered routing. We address both problems with the Conservative Hybrid Graph Network (CHGN), which learns routing, regime assignment, and removal rates as data-driven surrogates and inserts them into a fixed transport equation, so that the mass balance holds by construction for any predicted routing. CHGN trained on networks of 10-20 nodes transfers zero-shot to unseen graphs of 25-40 nodes without retraining, reaching an RMSE of 2.1e-3 against 6e-2 to 9e-2 for GNN baselines under the same protocol, with a gate MAE of 7.9e-3 and regime accuracy of 94.3% (1.2e-2 and 96.4% respectively on the fixed training topology). On a fluid-mixing pilot plant, CHGN improves on a persistence baseline for held-out physical faults but does not predict manual interventions, for which the governing valve actions are unobserved. The model therefore transfers across process topologies without retraining and exposes the latent mechanisms governing plant behaviour to inspection.

</details>


### [97] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Semantic ID Recommenders: Does the Model's Own Code Hierarchy Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28905)
*Artem Betlei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了生成推荐系统中语义ID（SID）树在离线策略评估（OPE）中的应用。研究发现，虽然单个物品的OPE因有效样本量过小而不可行，但通过将物品聚类到代码前缀组（code-prefix clusters）可显著提升估计性能。这种改进源于粗粒度化，而非层次结构本身；而SID树使得粗粒度化在生成系统中变得可行，因为解码器能高效精确地返回每个簇的质量。此外，分辨率深度是关键调节参数，在数据支持不足时应采用更粗的粒度，并且论文给出了条件偏差上界，其与量化器的最大重构残差及目标-日志分布差异相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成推荐系统中，为减少昂贵的A/B测试成本，需要在离线环境中评估不同解码器或重排序策略的性能。传统方法在物品级别进行离线策略评估（OPE）受限于生产日志中有效样本量小的问题，导致估计不准确。因此，亟需一种更有效的抽象方式来提升离线评估的可靠性。

Method: 提出利用生成推荐系统中固有的语义ID（SID）树结构作为动作抽象，将物品按代码前缀聚类形成簇，从而扩大有效样本支持。通过分析聚类后的估计误差和偏差，验证该方法在数据稀疏情况下的有效性，并推导出与量化器重建残差和目标-日志分布差异相关的条件偏差上界。

Result: 实验与理论分析表明，将物品聚类至代码前缀组可显著提高离线策略评估的估计稳定性与准确性；粗粒度化带来的收益主要来自样本量的提升，而非层级结构本身；而SID树的结构特性使得这种聚类计算高效、精确，优于传统的扁平聚类方法。分辨率深度的选择影响评估效果，较粗的粒度在数据稀缺时表现更优。

Conclusion: 生成推荐系统中的语义ID树可以作为有效的动作抽象用于离线策略评估。通过将物品聚类到代码前缀组，能够恢复可估计的支持集并降低估计误差。该方法的可行性依赖于SID树提供的高效质量计算能力，而粗粒度化带来的性能提升与量化器的重建能力及策略差异密切相关。

Abstract: Generative recommenders increasingly emit semantic IDs (SIDs): each item is a short sequence of hierarchical discrete codes from a residual quantizer, decoded autoregressively. Before spending scarce A/B-test, a team may decide offline which decoder or reranking variants are worth testing - a job for off-policy evaluation (OPE). We ask a simple question: can the model's own SID tree serve as the action abstraction for that OPE? Our answer has three parts. (i) Under the near-argmax logging real recommenders use, per-item OPE is hopeless - as item-level effective sample size is usually small on production logs - but marginalizing items to code-prefix clusters restores estimable support and cuts error. (ii) This gain is thanks to coarsening, not to the hierarchy specifically; but the SID tree is what makes coarsening feasible in a generative system - each cluster's mass is exactly and cheaply returned by the decoder, whereas flat clustering requires enumerating item/leaf masses that a code-only decoder does not directly expose. (iii) Resolution depth is the operative knob - coarser under scarce support - and a conditional bias bound links the coarsening bias to the quantizer's worst-case reconstruction residual and the target-logging divergence.

</details>


### [98] [Learning-Theoretic Foundation for General Coded Computing: The Straggler Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28910)
*Parsa Moradi,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为通用编码计算（General Coded Computing, GCC）的新框架，旨在解决现有编码计算方法在现代机器学习任务（如深度神经网络）中因依赖精确恢复和严格恢复阈值而适用性受限的问题。GCC从学习理论视角出发，以均方误差作为端到端损失函数，通过将编码器和解码器限制在具有平滑性约束的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）内，推导出其系数可高效计算的线性组合表示。该框架在最坏情况和概率性场景下均提供了理论性能保证：在最多S个慢节点的最坏情况下，端到端损失至少以$O(S^3N^{-3})$速率下降；在每个节点独立以概率p发生延迟的概率模型下，期望损失仍可收敛至$O(\log_{1/p}^3(N)N^{-3})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码计算方案主要针对具有严格代数结构的计算任务（如多项式求值、矩阵乘法），依赖精确恢复和固定恢复阈值，难以适用于缺乏结构化的现代机器学习任务（如深度神经网络），这些任务通常只需近似结果而非精确解。因此需要一种更灵活、适应性强的编码计算框架。

Method: 提出基于学习理论的通用编码计算（GCC）框架，采用端到端均方误差损失函数，将编码器与解码器限制在具有平滑性约束的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中，利用核函数的线性表示形式实现高效系数求解，并通过理论分析建立在最坏情况和概率性慢节点场景下的性能边界。

Result: 在最坏情况（最多S个慢节点）下，端到端损失衰减率不低于$O(S^3N^{-3})$；在概率性慢节点模型（每节点以概率p延迟）下，期望损失收敛速率可达$O(\log_{1/p}^3(N)N^{-3})$，证明了GCC在复杂实际环境中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GCC为非结构化、近似需求的机器学习计算提供了一个理论坚实且实用的编码计算新范式，突破了传统代数编码方法的局限，显著拓展了编码计算在现代分布式学习系统中的应用前景。

Abstract: Coded computing has emerged as a powerful paradigm for mitigating the impact of straggling workers in distributed computing systems. However, existing coded-computing schemes are predominantly designed for the exact recovery of highly structured computations, such as polynomial evaluation and matrix multiplication, and typically rely on strict recovery thresholds. These assumptions significantly limit their applicability to modern machine-learning workloads, particularly deep neural networks (DNNs), whose computations generally lack rigid algebraic structure and, in many applications, require only accurate approximations rather than exact recovery.
  To address this gap, we revisit coded computing from a learning-theoretic perspective and introduce General Coded Computing (GCC). Rather than adopting existing algebraic tools, GCC formulates coded computing through a natural end-to-end mean-squared error loss that directly measures the discrepancy between the desired computations and their recovered estimates. By deriving suitable upper bounds and restricting the encoder and decoder to a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) with mild smoothness constraints, we show that both the encoder and decoder admit specific representations as linear combinations of RKHS kernel functions. This representation allows the corresponding coefficients to be computed efficiently. Moreover, this framework enables us to establish theoretical performance guarantees for GCC under two complementary straggler regimes. In the worst-case setting with $N$ worker nodes, and at most $S$ stragglers, we show that the end-to-end loss decays at least at rate $O(S^3N^{-3})$ for standard configurations. We then study a probabilistic setting in which each worker independently straggles with probability $p$. We prove that the expected loss can still converge at rate $O(\log_{1/p}^3(N)N^{-3})$.

</details>


### [99] [SemKV: Semantic Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization Guided by the Quality Cliff for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28911)
*Daeha Lee,Do-Hyung Kim,Jae-Hong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了长上下文大语言模型推理中键值（KV）缓存的内存瓶颈，发现均匀的分数比特量化在特定统计协议下并非平滑退化：当精度降至2.322比特/值时仍无显著质量下降，但在2.0比特以下出现质量骤降（质量悬崖），且该现象在生成阶段、多轮对话中持续存在，并可迁移至Mistral-7B模型。研究提出SemKV方法，通过模型内部评分对所有token排序并分配两个相邻于悬崖之上的精度，实现6.0倍存储压缩且质量无统计差异（n=900，三组种子）。相比赋予1.5倍更大内存预算的FP16剪枝，SemKV表现更优。进一步使用优化失真度的量化器（TurboQuant-MSE）可降低悬崖阈值，将无损失操作点提升至7.9倍。核心策略：针对部署环境测量悬崖位置，然后在悬崖之上进行插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是长上下文大语言模型推理中的主要内存瓶颈，其大小随上下文长度线性增长。现有均匀量化方法在低比特下无法平滑退化，存在突然的质量下降（质量悬崖），限制了高效压缩的可能性。因此需要理解并克服这一非连续性问题，以实现更高效、稳定的低比特量化。

Method: 采用分数比特网格上的均匀量化，结合多种子统计协议评估量化性能；提出SemKV方法，基于模型内部重要性评分对所有token排序，分配两个高于质量悬崖的相邻精度；引入畸变优化量化器（TurboQuant-MSE）以降低悬崖阈值；通过实证分析确定最优量化策略——在悬崖之上进行插值。

Result: SemKV实现6.0倍存储压缩，且与全精度KV在统计上无差异（n=900，三组种子）；优于给予1.5倍内存预算的FP16 token pruning；使用TurboQuant-MSE后，无损失操作点提升至7.9倍；验证了八种内部重要性指标在悬崖之上具有等效性，支持混合精度设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 质量悬崖是低比特KV量化中的关键障碍，但可通过识别其位置并在其之上进行插值来规避。SemKV结合重要性排序与双精度分配，实现了高效率压缩而无质量损失。未来应优先测量部署场景下的悬崖阈值，再设计插值策略以最大化压缩比与稳定性。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the dominant memory bottleneck of long-context large language model (LLM) inference, growing linearly with context length. We show that uniform KV quantization on a fractional-bit grid does not degrade gracefully: under a prespecified multi-seed statistical protocol, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with an affine quantizer is statistically indistinguishable from FP16 KV down to 2.322 code bits/value and collapses at 2.0 bits - a quality cliff in (2.0, 2.322] that reappears in generation-time quantization and multi-turn dialogue and transfers to Mistral-7B. The cliff reframes importance-aware mixed precision: above it, eight model-internal importance indicators are statistically interchangeable, so the benefit of mixing is grid interpolation, reaching average precisions uniform quantization cannot realize. SemKV preserves every token, ranks tokens by a model-internal score, and assigns two adjacent above-cliff precisions, achieving a measured 6.0x storage reduction with no statistically detectable quality difference from full KV (n=900, three seeds), and outperforming FP16 token pruning granted a 1.5x larger memory budget. Replacing the affine base with a distortion-optimized quantizer (TurboQuant-MSE) lowers the cliff in every protocol tested, raising the no-detectable-loss operating point to 7.9x. The recipe: measure the cliff for the target deployment setting, then interpolate above it.

</details>


### [100] [RankShift: In-Database Detection and Explanation of Categorical Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28922)
*Omair Shafi Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RankShift 是一种无需模型训练或推理服务的异常检测方法，通过比较数据窗口中各类别占比与良性参考基准的 Pearson 分数来检测类别分布偏移。它能有效识别罕见类别比例变化，即使事件总数不变，在 HDFS、BGL 和 Thunderbird 数据集上表现优于或媲美基于计数向量的自编码器，且具有更低的误报率和更小的部署开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统日志等场景中，某些罕见事件模板可能从低频变为高频，但整体消息速率保持稳定，导致事件总数不变，仅类别分布发生变化。传统基于事件计数的监控方法难以察觉此类变化，因此需要一种能够检测类别分布偏移的方法。

Method: RankShift 采用 Pearson 相关系数计算每个时间窗口内类别占比与良性参考基准之间的差异，利用其分项识别出导致分布变化的关键类别，并返回得分、校准警报及最大增长贡献项。该方法完全基于查询执行，无需训练或部署复杂模型。

Result: 在 HDFS、BGL、Thunderbird 数据集上的实验表明，RankShift 在 AUROC 指标上达到 0.999（HDFS）、0.983（Thunderbird），优于或媲美自编码器方法；在固定数据量的控制实验中，其对罕见类别偏移的检测能力（AUROC=0.787）优于自编码器（0.771）。所有数据集的误报率均符合预设操作水平。

Conclusion: RankShift 能高效检测类别分布偏移，无需模型训练，部署轻量，性能优越，适用于实时日志分析等场景中的异常检测任务。

Abstract: A login service can receive its usual number of failed sign-ins while one source grows from 2% to 30% of them. The same pattern appears in system logs when a rare event template becomes common while the message rate stays stable. These events change which categories are active without changing how many events occur. RankShift detects such changes inside the analytical database that stores the data. It compares each window's category shares with a benign reference using a Pearson score whose terms identify the categories responsible for the change. The same query returns the score, calibrated alert, and largest increasing contributions. We evaluate RankShift on HDFS, BGL, and Thunderbird. It matches the count-vector autoencoder within 0.001 AUROC on HDFS (0.999 versus 1.000) and leads on Thunderbird (0.983 versus 0.949). In a controlled fixed-volume experiment, RankShift detects rare-category shifts that are invisible to event-count monitoring, reaching 0.787 AUROC compared with 0.771 for the autoencoder. Across all three corpora, observed false-alarm rates track the requested operating levels. RankShift requires no model training or inference service, and the autoencoders deployed state is 137x larger.

</details>
